Wie können wir Ihnen helfen?[](https://help.impact.com/home/de#wie-konnen-wir-ihnen-helfen)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Drücken Sie Strg + K oder Cmd + K, um zu beginnen!

[](https://help.impact.com/brand/de/)

# 登录

欢迎回来！

欢迎回来！
=====

输入您的电子邮件地址或用户名或使用 SSO 登录。

电子邮箱地址或用户名继续

或

使用以下方式登录 Google使用以下方式登录 Apple使用以下方式登录 Facebook使用以下方式登录 LinkedIn使用以下方式登录 X

没有账户？注册为 [合作伙伴](https://help.impact.com/signup/none/create-new-mediapartner-account-flow.ihtml)
, [品牌](https://impact.com/integrated-platform-prices/?utm_source=website&utm_content=app_login)
, 或 [代理商](https://impact.com/agency-partners/)

[条款和隐私](https://impact.com/terms-of-use/)

# Manage Your Exception Lists | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/manage-your-exception-lists.md)
.

After you create a [category](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists#create-a-category-exception-list)
, [SKU](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-sku-exception-lists)
, or [promo code](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-promo-code-exception-lists)
 exception list, you can perform additional actions like editing, viewing, and downloading the list.

SKU and promo code [exception lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists)
 are not available in every impact.com edition. For example, Product-Led Growth (PLG) edition accounts do not include the **SKU** or **Promo Code** option under _Exception Lists_. If you do not see either option, you may need to upgrade your account. [Contact support](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/support/customer-support-portal-flow.ihtml)
.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the _Program_ column, scroll to _Exception Lists_ and select the exception list you want to manage.
    
    *   Once on the exception list screen, you can switch between exception lists by selecting the available tabs, such as **Exception Lists (Category)**, **SKU Exception Lists**, or **Promo Code Exception Lists**.
        
    
3.  Hover over the exception list you want to manage, then select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]**.
    
4.  Choose one of the following actions based on the exception list type:
    
    *   **Edit:** Change the exception list's _Name_, _Associated Event Type_, or _Match Expression_.
        
    *   **Add Items/Promo Codes:** Add more items or promo codes directly, or upload a CSV or Excel file.
        
    *   **View Items/Promo Codes:** View all the items or promo codes for a particular exception list.
        
    *   **Clear Items/Promo Codes:** Remove all the items or promo codes from your exception list.
        
    *   **Deactivate:** Deactivate your exception list. If you deactivate an exception list without removing it from your template terms, the exception list will still show in the template terms, but the payout rule won't apply to partners.
        
    *   **Download CSV:** Download the exception list as a CSV file.
        
    

[PreviousSet Payout Rules for Category, SKU, or Promo Code Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/set-payout-rules-for-exception-lists)
[NextCreate SKU Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-sku-exception-lists)

Last updated 23 days ago

Was this helpful?

# Getting Started with Starter Edition | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/getting-started-with-starter-edition.md)

# Set Payout Rules for Category, SKU, or Promo Code Exception Lists | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/set-payout-rules-for-exception-lists.md)
.

Once a category, SKU, or promo code exception list is active, you can add it to template terms under the **Payout Groups** option.

**Payout logic:** If a payout group's condition is met, it will apply the payout change at the same level (order or item) as the template term's default payout. For example, if your default payout applies a payout at the item level, specific items will have their payouts changed if they fall into the category entered in that payout group.

#### Set payout rules for exception lists[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/set-payout-rules-for-exception-lists#set-payout-rules-for-exception-lists)

SKU and promo code [exception lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists)
 are not available in every impact.com edition. For example, Product-Led Growth (PLG) edition accounts do not include the **SKU** or **Promo Code** option under _Exception Lists_. If you do not see either option, you may need to upgrade your account. [Contact support](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/support/customer-support-portal-flow.ihtml)
.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Contracts → Template Terms**.
    
2.  Hover over the template terms to which you want to add the exception list, then select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]→ Modify Terms**.
    
    *   If you want to create new template terms, select **Create Template Terms** in the upper right corner.
        
    
3.  Next to _Payout Groups_, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]**, then select **Add New**.
    
4.  From the first ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** next to _Rules_, select one of the following options:
    
    *   List for Order Promo Code
        
    *   List for Item Category
        
    *   List for Item SKU
        
    
    If your edition does not include SKU or promo code exception lists, those rule options will not appear.
    
5.  Continue the rule configuration by selecting **Is** as the operator and the value of the exception list item. The names of the lists will auto-populate based on what is already set up.
    
    *   E.g. `List for Item Category` `is` `Apparel`
        
    
6.  Set the **Payout** you want for items on the list.
    
7.  Select **Add** to complete the process.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FjV1iknQiPylYK2ZmtxZj%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-23%2520at%252011.55.58.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D623f97e3-9889-4dfc-b985-0fd009a318aa&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ea186079d40cc649b53504843a5ad01b&sv=3)

**Note:** You can view and download these exception lists in the applicable template terms.

[PreviousCreate Category Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists)
[NextManage Your Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/manage-your-exception-lists)

# Step 1: Review & Activate Your Product Catalog Feed | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/im-new-to-impact.com/welcome-to-impact.com-starter-edition/step-1-review-and-activate-your-product-catalog-feed.md)
.

If you have a product catalog on an e-commerce site, you can configure a catalog. [Turn on product catalogs for your program](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/product-catalogs/add-product-catalogs-as-a-brand)
.

*   **Shopify:** [Connect your Shopify product catalog](https://integrations.impact.com/integration-guides/for-brands/plugin-integrations/e-commerce/integrate-with-shopify/shopify-product-catalog)
     to easily add an up-to-date list of your store's products.
    
*   **Big Commerce/Magento/Woo Commerce:** Manually sync your product feed via the [Integrated Shopping Cart Import](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/product-catalogs/add-product-catalogs-as-a-brand)
     (In step 6 of the _Add a catalog_ instructions, follow the instructions in the _Integrated Shopping Cart Import_ tab).

# Configure Gateway Tracking Settings | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings.md)
.

_Gateway Tracking Settings_ is where you can configure behavior for your impact.com tracking links, like your default landing page, [tracking templates](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates)
, SSL support, how partners can deep link to your website, and a couple of advanced options.

#### Configure Gateway Tracking Settings[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings#configure-gateway-tracking-settings)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the _Program Settings_ column (right), scroll down to _Tracking_ and select **Gateway**.
    

#### Modify a setting[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings#modify-a-setting)

1.  Select the setting that you want to change.
    
2.  Refer to the sections below for help with each setting, then make your changes.
    
3.  Select **Save**.
    

Set a default landing page[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings#set-a-default-landing-page)

Set the **default landing page** for all of your Gateway URLs (tracking links).

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FWEuuiXAX98sgzELMgNj5%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252016.09.51.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da25a8e40-738f-4289-9d70-45508543886b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8d898f67cff6f7659bc199df5c6ca8b4&sv=3)

*   If an ad with a custom landing page doesn't work, the tracking link will redirect to this landing page.
    
*   If you allow partners to create custom tracking links (e.g., short links or vanity links) via the Create a Link widget, this URL will be the default landing page for those tracking links.
    

SSL Support[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings#ssl-support)

This should be enabled by default and will ensure all users are routed to the secure version of your site (HTTPS) via tracking links.

In cases where a partner uses a secure tracking link and your site doesn't support SSL, when the user clicks a link, it will prompt a message stating that they are about to leave a secure site to visit a non-secure site. If your site supports both http:// and https:// and your site is secure, this message can be canceled by redirecting the user to your secure site.

**Warning:** If your site does not support HTTPS, the user will be redirected to a broken URL.

Deep Linking[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings#deep-linking)

This should be enabled by default and will allow users to be routed directly to pages on your site.

In order to enable partners to deep link to your site, you will need to input _Permitted Domains_. For more information on how to do this, see [Set Up Permitted Domains for Deep Linking](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-up-permitted-domains-for-deep-linking)
.

[PreviousSet Up Permitted Domains for Deep Linking](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-up-permitted-domains-for-deep-linking)
[NextGateway Tracking Templates](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates)

# Starter Edition for Brands Overview | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/starter-edition-for-brands-overview.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1248060?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=fund-154&utm_campaign=help-center)

The documents listed below provide everything you need to know about how to run a successful partnership program using impact.com’s Starter Edition. We suggest you begin by walking through all the steps outlined in [Getting Started for Starter Edition Brands](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/getting-started-with-starter-edition)
, so that you are set up for success.

# Review Data Connection Submissions | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/review-data-connection-submissions.md)
.

_Connectors_ are a system for all batch data file transfers between impact.com and brands, partners, or other parties. Each connection (an individual connector) has certain attributes, such as the data type (clicks, conversions, product catalogs, etc.), direction (inbound or outbound), method (SFTP, FTP, Email), and more.

You can view the history of files submitted via connections within your Account Settings.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, under _Technical_, select **Connections**.
    
3.  In the list of connections, hover your cursor over the connection you want to review submissions for and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-1e0bbc2fbb260c9846c479063dc2a10f301c5a3b%252Ffb08358c10eb18ae39cd472335b74bd43ce45f7c7e5ff705cd708d9d2b84d37d.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f33df08eeeec222cab108a0badb9bd6f&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **View History**.
    

From here, you can view and filter detailed information about each submission, including the date the file was received, whether the submission was successful, and if there were any errors in the file.

[PreviousConfigure a Data Download Connection](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection)
[NextImport Aggregate Performance Data from Other Networks](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/import-aggregate-performance-data-from-other-networks)

# Create Category Exception Lists | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117596?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=con-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

Category exception lists let you pay partners different rates based on the category associated with an action.

**When would I use this feature?**

*   **Lead Gen:** Category-based payouts may be implemented for lead generation campaigns that wish to vary payouts according to demographics. For example, your business may have a higher lead conversion rate for customers from the United States as compared to other countries. You could then decide to assign a higher category payout to leads for customers in the US.
    

Create a category exception list[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists#create-a-category-exception-list)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the _Program_ column, scroll to _Exception Lists_ and select [**Category**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/campaign/itemlist/view-categoryitemlist-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  In the upper right corner, select **Add New Category List**.
    
4.  Fill out the following information:
    
    Field
    
    Description
    
    **List Name**
    
    Add a name for this list—this will only appear internally to you and other account members; partners will not see it.
    
    **Associate Event Type**
    
    Select 1 or more event types to associate with this exception list.
    
    **Match Expression**
    
    **\[Advanced & Optional\]** Enables the system to match a part of the category value via specifying a regular expression with 1 grouping. The value represented by the grouping will be matched against the category value in this list. If you have a use case for regex but are unsure how to configure it, [contact support](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml?createTicket=true&)
    .
    
5.  Select **Save and Add Items** to continue.
    
6.  You can add **Categories** and optional **Display Values** using the fields in the UI, or you can **Upload a CSV or Excel file** with your categories.
    
7.  Once you've added items or uploaded a file, select **Save** to complete the process. You'll now see your new category exception list on the _Category Lists_ screen.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FvD0JEUv4z5YD7AUe5UWk%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252018.06.48.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D62ec3787-adaa-40af-b107-262e328037d0&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=75d0779b52131545d965ef5c47052135&sv=3)

Category exception list format[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists#category-exception-list-format)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Create a .CSV or .XLS/.XLSX file using the two column names below.

Column

Type

Required

Description

Category

String

**Yes**

The value that is included in tracking conversion data.

Display Value

String

No

Enter a display value if you want to show a different value in reporting. For example, if the Category value is some sort of computer generated value, then you might want to enter a display value to make it easier to understand in reporting.

**For example:**

Typically, payout rates are based on item categories; however, you can also set a payout rate for a specific item. You would need to create an item category for each item you want to have a specific payout. When you create template terms, you'll need to specify payout rates for each category list. All category names associated with a category list will have the same payout rate per the contract.

[PreviousCreate Promo Code Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-promo-code-exception-lists)
[NextSet Payout Rules for Category, SKU, or Promo Code Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/set-payout-rules-for-exception-lists)

*   [Category exception list format](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists#category-exception-list-format)

# Invite an Agency Account User | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/invite-an-agency-account-user.md)
.

**Note:** You'll need the agency's Account Id in order to invite them to manage your account. [Contact support](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml?createTicket=true)
 (or reach out to your CSM) to discuss inviting an agency to manage your account.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the left side under _General_, select **Account Users**.
    
3.  In the upper-right corner of the screen, select **Invite**, then select **Invite Agency**.
    
4.  Add the **Agency Id** or optionally invite an agency user by entering their email address.
    
5.  From the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**, assign the agency to a **User Access Group** or **Create new user access** for the particular agency.
    
6.  Optionally, you can include an **Invitation Message** with your invite.
    
7.  Select **Invite** to send the invitation to the agency.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F4JUcTIMolk1Z4hWgTYBr%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-16%2520at%252010.42.45%2520%282%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddce24fe9-4ba3-4303-9f6e-298de4c70e1c&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=35e726cdcbd4cb98cc839cc7b91035f1&sv=3)

# Why Has My Account Been Suspended? | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-your-account/why-has-my-account-been-suspended.md)
.

impact.com automatically suspends brand accounts that are more than 40 days overdue on either impact.com fee payments or partner payments.

What suspension means for you[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-your-account/why-has-my-account-been-suspended#what-suspension-means-for-you)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   You have limited access to the impact.com platform. You can only log in, [contact support](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml?createTicket=true)
    , and [deposit funds into your funding account](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/add-funds-to-your-funding-account/deposit-funds-into-your-account)
    .
    
*   Your account operations such as tracking, reversals, subscriptions, and FTPs will continue to work while your account is suspended.
    

How to regain full account access[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-your-account/why-has-my-account-been-suspended#how-to-regain-full-account-access)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   [Make a deposit](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/add-funds-to-your-funding-account/deposit-funds-into-your-account)
     to your impact.com account that is sufficient to cover the full overdue balance.
    
*   As soon as the payment has been applied to your funding account, access to the platform will be automatically restored.
    

Consequences of extended failure to pay[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-your-account/why-has-my-account-been-suspended#consequences-of-extended-failure-to-pay)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Further payment delays may result in your account being referred to collections.
    
*   Your account will be at risk of deactivation.
    

[PreviousHow Do I Close My Brand Account?](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-your-account/how-do-i-close-my-brand-account)
[NextAPI Tokens](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens)

# Invite Account Users | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/invite-account-users.md)
.

This article will guide you on how to invite your colleagues (or an [agency](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/invite-an-agency-account-user)
) to your impact.com brand account. Learn how to manage existing account users through [user access groups](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-user-access-groups)
.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  Under _General_, select **Account Users**.
    
3.  In the upper-right corner of the screen, select **Invite** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** → **Invite User**.
    
4.  Below _User Email_, enter one or more user email addresses.
    
    *   Multiple email addresses are comma-separated.
        
    
5.  In the _User Access Group_ section, apply a group from the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** or select **Create new user access** to create a new one.
    
6.  Optionally, send an invitation message to the user.
    
    *   Select **Preview message** if you want to view the invitation before sending it.
        
    
7.  Select **Invite** to send the invitation to the invitee.
    
    *   An invitation email will then be received in the invitee's email box where they will accept your invitation.
        
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fe9OIJyM3TWyF3vBQHjwZ%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-16%2520at%252010.37.25%2520%282%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7b066767-109c-4440-aef3-e73e5ba28ab3&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b4de326f75dfe856e39f21a4748fa66a&sv=3)

# Create SKU Exception Lists | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-sku-exception-lists.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117596?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=con-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

SKU exception lists let you pay partners different rates based on the SKU associated with an action.

SKU and promo code [exception lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists)
 are not available in every impact.com edition. For example, Product-Led Growth (PLG) edition accounts do not include the **SKU** or **Promo Code** option under _Exception Lists_. If you do not see either option, you may need to upgrade your account. [Contact support](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/support/customer-support-portal-flow.ihtml)
.

Create a SKU exception list[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-sku-exception-lists#create-a-sku-exception-list)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the _Program_ column, scroll to _Exception Lists_ and select [**SKU**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/campaign/itemlist/view-skuitemlist-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  In the upper right corner, select **Add New SKU List**.
    
4.  Fill out the following information:
    
    Field
    
    Description
    
    **List Name**
    
    Add a name for this list—this will only appear internally to you and other account members; partners will not see it.
    
    **Associate Event Type**
    
    Select 1 or more event types to associate with this exception list.
    
    **Match Expression**
    
    **\[Advanced & Optional\]** Enables the system to match only on a part of the SKU value via specifying a regular expression with 1 grouping. The value represented by the grouping will be matched against the SKU value in this list. If you have a use case for regex but are unsure how to configure it, contact support.
    
5.  Select **Save and Add Items** to continue.
    
6.  You can add a **SKU** and optional **Display Values** using the fields in the UI, or you can **Upload a CSV or Excel file** with your SKUs.
    
7.  Once you've added items or uploaded a file, select **Save** to complete the process. You'll now see your new SKU exception list on the _SKU Lists_ screen.
    

SKU exception list format[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-sku-exception-lists#sku-exception-list-format)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Create a .CSV or .XLS/.XLSX file using the column names below.

Column

Type

Required

Description

SKU

String

Yes

The value that is included in tracking conversion data.

Display Value

String

No

Enter a display value if you want to show a different value in reporting. For example, if the Category value is some sort of computer-generated value, then you might want to enter a display value to make it easier to understand in reporting.

Active Start Date

Date

No

Gives you the ability to schedule this item. This is an ISO 8601 format. Example format: 2038-01-19T12:00:00-07:00

Active End Date

Date

Only if _Start Date_ is provided

Gives you the ability to schedule this item. This is an ISO 8601 format. Example format: 2038-01-19T23:59:59-07:00

When setting end dates to SKUs, any SKUs that expire (passes the set end date) will not trigger any payouts stipulated in the contract. Be sure to remove expired SKUs to avoid payouts from not triggering as stipulated in your terms.

**Example**: If you want to run a promotion for a limited time on selected SKUs, you can create a SKU exception list and schedule times for when the promotion is valid for on each related SKU. Then you can set a payout group to pay the different payouts on the various SKUs. If an order with a SKU on this list is tracked outside of the scheduled time, it will not trigger the promotional payout.

Timezone

String

No

Timezone is derived from the start date, but if you want to override it with a specific timezone, enter the full name here, e.g. "America/New\_York"

**For example:**

*   [SKU exception list format](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-sku-exception-lists#sku-exception-list-format)

# Control User Sign-up Methods | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/control-user-sign-up-methods.md)
.

You can set how users may sign up to join your account and define user sign-up authorization and authentication methods.

**Note**: Only users with [Account Security Settings permissions](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/understanding-user-management-as-a-brand)
 can access the _User Signup Method_ section.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the left, under the _General_ section, select [**Account User Authentication**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/accountSettings/account-security-settings-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  In the _User Signup Method_ section, select the preferred method you'd like the user to sign up with when joining your account.
    

Single sign-on (SSO)

SAML

1.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FwXho9QKP3e4iU4ZKUSJ5%252F167ebb8cc30303.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da046e9f0-63aa-46f6-8a35-f73973fd326c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da58aac3e10a80e67d81ac893e14f6f6&sv=3) **\[Checkbox\]** **Single sign-on (SSO)**.
    
2.  Select one or multiple sign-up methods:
    
    *   Apple
        
    *   Google
        
    

Refer to [Enable SAML Single Sign-On](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/enable-saml-single-sign-on)
 for more information on using this method.

1.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FwXho9QKP3e4iU4ZKUSJ5%252F167ebb8cc30303.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da046e9f0-63aa-46f6-8a35-f73973fd326c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da58aac3e10a80e67d81ac893e14f6f6&sv=3) **\[Checkbox\]** **SAML**.
    
2.  Select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**, then select your preferred option:
    
    1.  Impact
        
    2.  AD FS
        
    3.  Microsoft Entra ID
        
    4.  Okta
        
    5.  Onelogin
        
    

**Important**: Users that have joined your account using the _SAML_ method cannot be reverted to the _Username & Password_ method.

1.  Select **Save**.

# Create a User Access Group | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/create-a-user-access-group.md)
.

Before inviting users to your brand account, you need to set up user roles and _Access Groups_. This allows you to add new users to pre-defined groups with permissions tailored to their specific roles.

**Important:** Before creating an access group, make sure you have the _User Role_ you want to assign to that group. For more details on the full process, see [Understanding User Management as a Brand](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/understanding-user-management-as-a-brand)
.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the left, under _General_, select **User Access Groups**.
    
3.  Select **Create User Access Group**.
    
4.  Enter a _Name_ for the user access group.
    
5.  ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** the product type to enable user access to it (e.g., Performance, Altitude).
    
    1.  From the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**, select the **Programs** for which you want to allow access to the product type to (e.g., _Performance_, _Altitude_).
        
    2.  From the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**, assign the appropriate **Role** to the access group.
        
        **Note:** You can assign predefined impact.com user roles or create custom user roles.
        
        *   Optionally, select **View Permissions**, to see the permissions associated with the selected role for the user.
            
        
    
6.  Select **Save**.
    
    *   Optionally, select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]** to **Set as default user access group**. If you enable this setting, the _User Access Group_ will be the default when inviting new users.
        
    

[PreviousManage Custom Roles](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-custom-roles)
[NextManage User Access Groups](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-user-access-groups)

# Set Your Initial Finance Settings | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/set-your-initial-finance-settings.md)
.

[Take the PXA course (associate level)](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1087854?debug=true?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=tra-200&utm_campaign=help-center)
[Take the PXA course (expert level)](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117597?debug=true?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=tra-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

During your onboarding, you'll set up the initial finance settings for your program. These settings control when actions that partners drive will lock and when they should receive their commissions.

impact.com recommends the configuration outlined below as a good starting point for your brand. Always defer to your onboarding team for help if you need to customize your setup further.

1

**Step 1: Configure Statement of Invoice (SOI) settings**

The SOI coincides with an invoicing strategy—all of your obligations for a given billing period are tallied up in the SOI, and you'll deposit funds into your account so they're settled by the due date specified.

You should set your SOI settings so that the document only includes a tally of partner invoices and so that all of your Finance contacts receive the document when it is generated. Learn how to [configure your SOI settings](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/add-funds-to-your-funding-account/manage-funding-account-document-settings-as-a-brand)
.

**Invoice prioritization:** You should fund your account to cover both your impact.com invoices and the SOI. If you do not fund your account enough to cover both, funds will first go towards settling your impact.com invoice, which might lead to overdue partner invoices. Learn more about [how invoices are prioritized](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/finance-explained-for-brands)
.

2

**Step 2: Set Action Locking/Payment Scheduling Periods**

Learn how to [set up action locking/payment scheduling periods](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/set-action-locking-and-payment-scheduling-periods)
. We recommend that you set actions to lock 27 days after the end of the month they are tracked, and schedule payments to go out 20 days after the end of the month the actions lock.

3

**Step 3: Add a primary Finance Contact**

Finance Contacts will receive all financial documents and notifications from impact.com.

You likely will have already provided this information to impact.com. Just to be safe, double-check that what you have listed is still up-to-date.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select **your balance \[Drop-down menu\] → Settings → General**.
    
2.  In the _Finance Contacts_ section, under _Primary Contact_, select either:
    
    *   **Existing Account User:** If your finance contact already has an impact.com account and has it set up, select the contact from the drop-down menu. This user needs to be granted the [Finance permission](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/understanding-user-management-as-a-brand)
        , which gives them access to all finance actions.
        
    *   **Other Contact:** If your finance contact has not set up an impact.com account yet, enter your contact's information.
        
    
3.  Select **Save**.
    

4

**Step 4: Optionally, add a Vendor Code**

If your accounting team requires a vendor code to appear on your invoices, then you can set one up from the _Billing Groups_ screen.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select **your balance \[Drop-down menu\] → Settings → Billing Groups**.
    
2.  Hover over the applicable billing group then select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **impact.com Fees Document Settings**.
    
3.  Enter the desired **Vendor Code**.
    
4.  Select **Save**.
    

5

**Step 5: Submit your billing address**

The last step in setting up your finance settings is to provide your billing address to impact.com. This is the address that will be listed on all your invoices from impact.com.

You will likely have already provided your address under your [Account Information](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/accountSettings/account-info-flow.ihtml)
, but you may wish to provide an alternate address for bills as follows:

1.  From the top navigation bar, select **your balance \[Drop-down menu\] → Settings → Legal Entities**.
    
2.  Hover over the legal entity for whom you'd like to change the address, then select **Edit**.
    
3.  In the _Billing Address_ section, update or provide the relevant address details.
    
4.  Select **Save**.
    

[PreviousGetting Started with Starter Edition](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/getting-started-with-starter-edition)
[NextSet Action Locking & Payment Scheduling Periods](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/set-action-locking-and-payment-scheduling-periods)

Last updated 17 days ago

# Set Up Permitted Domains for Deep Linking | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-up-permitted-domains-for-deep-linking.md)
.

If you want partners to be able to deep link to specific pages on your website, you must provide impact.com with the domains to which partners can send traffic (impact.com refers to these as "permitted domains"). You should do this regardless of whether you know what specific domains you want traffic arriving at or not.

#### Using non-permitted domains[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-up-permitted-domains-for-deep-linking#using-non-permitted-domains)

If partners don't use the permitted domains you provide in their deep links, all traffic they drive using that link will be sent to a generic impact.com page with no options to head to your website. If you aren't sure what domains to list here, reach out to your teammates who manage your website. If they aren't sure or if there are no specific domains they come back with, you should enter the defaults (which are provided below).

#### Set permitted domains[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-up-permitted-domains-for-deep-linking#set-permitted-domains)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, under _Tracking_, select **Gateway**.
    
3.  Next to _Deep Linking_, toggle on ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **Allow Partners to deep link to specific pages on my site**.
    
4.  Under _Permitted Domains_, if you don't have specific domains to add, enter the following default domains:
    
    Domain
    
    Example
    
    \[Your website domain\].\[Your website top-level domain\]
    
    impact.com
    
    \*.\[Your website domain\].\[Your website top-level domain\]
    
    \*.impact.com
    
5.  Alternatively, if you do have your own permitted domains to enter here, follow these formatting guidelines:
    
    *   Do not include any paths or slashes (`/`) in your permitted domain. Exclude anything including and after the slash following the URL's domain.
        
    *   If a URL might include a subdomain (e.g., `www.`) when used to deep link, you must include both versions of the URL. In this case, follow the format that the default permitted domains (above) use.
        
    *   Don't include the default permitted domains (above) if you provide your own specific domains.
        
    
6.  Select **Save**.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fp5z9a3MX6S1VkLI6N87Z%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252016.03.22.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D72bd5d56-010a-49ef-b843-82ee527b44f6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ff1f36fdabfeac11baafe4f54614cea6&sv=3)

#### Fix common partner-facing error messages[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-up-permitted-domains-for-deep-linking#fix-common-partner-facing-error-messages)

Example 1

Example 2

**Error message:** The link you clicked on is malformed. Contact the editor of the originating page.

This error could be because you don't have deep linking enabled. To enable it, do the following:

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, in the the _Tracking_ section, select **Gateway**.
    
3.  In the _Deep Linking_ section select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fkka3dqJOx8ZhiX82RDLt%252FEdit_light.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df454405c-ebd8-49af-90db-22efc27476bf&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9628ee367a677342e943e90378c7c017&sv=3) **\[Edit\]** and ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** **Allow Partners to deep link to specific pages on my site**.
    
4.  Specify the base domains to which you allow partners to deep link.
    
    *   Domains should be comma-separated with no spaces. Wildcard characters are supported. E.g., yoursite.,\*.yoursite.
        
    
5.  Select **Save**.
    

Alternatively, you may not have block rules set up. To check whether you do:

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, in the _Technical_ section, select [Blocking & Redirect](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/tracking-settings/manage-block-redirect-rules-flow.ihtml?)
    .
    
3.  Ensure that you have a block rule set up. Learn more on how to [Configure Block Routing Rules](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/blocking-and-redirect-rules/configure-block-routing-rules)
    .
    

**Error message:** The link you clicked on has expired. We apologize for any inconvenience.

The partner may be blocklisted from your program. If you want to remove the partner from the blocklist, [contact support](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/support/customer-support-portal-flow.ihtml)
.

# Create & Manage General Exception Lists | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists.md)
.

General lists let you group values associated with an attribute so you can affect payments for all of them at the same rate. For example, if you want to increase the payout by 10% for certain customer cities, add those cities to the same list, then reference the list in your [payout rules](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/contracts-and-template-terms/template-terms/payout-adjustments)
 instead of configuring a separate rule for each city.

Create & map a general exception list[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists#create-and-map-a-general-exception-list)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Creating a general list is a three-step flow: first, you define the list, add its values, then reference the list in a payout rule.

1

### Create the list[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists#create-the-list)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, under _Exception Lists_, select [**General**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/campaign/itemlist/view-generalitemlist-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  Enter a name for the list in **List Name**.
    
4.  Under **Associate Event Type**, select the event type to associate with the exception list.
    
5.  Optionally, select **Show advanced settings** to set a match expression. Enter a regular expression to match specific patterns.
    
6.  Select **Save and Add Items** to continue and add values to the list, **Save** to save the list without adding items yet, or **Cancel** to discard the list.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F35MX3vz5VRRu9vCDkAvd%252FScreenshot%25202026-07-20%2520at%252013.28.34.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da000a259-d8a3-4695-9a93-0276ca7e76c6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c77ae0df175fb454bb6fe18b27bb6da7&sv=3)

2

### Add values to the list[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists#add-values-to-the-list)

1.  After you select **Save and Add Items**, the _\[List Name\] : Add General Values_ screen opens. Under _Add Value Items_, add values one of two ways:
    
    *   **Enter values manually:** enter a _Value_ and, optionally, a _Display Value_, then select **Add Item** to add another row.
        
    *   **Upload a file:** under _Upload CSV or Excel File_, select **Choose file** and select a `.csv` or Excel file. Select **Show/hide file format details** to review the required file format.
        
    
2.  Select **Save** to save the values, or **Cancel** to discard them.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fo969v8Hspqv1nIlAS22A%252FScreenshot%25202026-07-20%2520at%252013.30.40.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8787892d-5354-4ae9-8d39-14c700d22dc9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8d6a229cc6c12b329d2e5c6ddec71086&sv=3)

3

### Reference a list in a payout rule[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists#reference-a-list-in-a-payout-rule)

1.  [Modify](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/contracts-and-template-terms/template-terms/modify-template-terms)
     or [create](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/contracts-and-template-terms/template-terms/create-template-terms)
     the template term where you want to use the general exception list.
    
2.  Next to the payout modification, configure the payout rule by adding the attribute associated with the exception list, for example `Customer City`.
    
3.  In the rule's operator dropdown menu, select `in list`, `not in list`, or `when present is not in list`, depending on which conversions you want the rule to affect.
    
    *   `in list`: The rule applies when the attribute's value is found in the list you select.
        
    *   `not in list`: The rule applies when the attribute's value is not found in the list you select.
        
    *   `when present is not in list`: The rule applies only when the attribute has a value, and that value isn't found in the list you select. Use this if you want to leave conversions untouched when the attribute is missing entirely, rather than treating a missing value the same as one that doesn't match.
        
    

**Note:** Date, money, and numeric attributes don't support `in list`, `not in list`, and `when present is not in list`.

1.  In the text box, add the general exception list by typing the list's name.
    
2.  Select **Add**.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMJbBWqRDrjRFUYuVy3nX%252FScreenshot%25202026-07-20%2520at%252012.28.55.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D082e754f-83c2-4abf-98c9-0ca34c9455ef&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5b2cecfacb218c87eef909e4770e4c26&sv=3)

Update, view, or remove a general list[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists#update-view-or-remove-a-general-list)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**Note:** When you update a general list, the payout rule referencing the list will use the updated version.

1.  On the _General Exception Lists_ screen, hover over the general list you want to update, then select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FPORUgP0qyEEctyFkbw08%252Factions.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2d406300-0455-4291-8028-aed6300b6e3c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0154d8e72cfe696c92f3a0f6c925d560&sv=3) **\[More\]**.
    
2.  Choose your action:
    
    *   **Edit:** Update the list's name, associated event type, or match expression.
        
    *   **Add Value:** Add more values to the list, either manually or by file upload.
        
    *   **View Values:** View the list's current values without downloading it.
        
    *   **Clear Values:** Remove all values currently in the list.
        
    *   **Deactivate:** Stop the list from being available for use, without deleting it. When deactivated, the list's _status_ will update to `Deactivated`.
        
        *   You can either reactivate or delete the deactivated list.
            
        
    *   **Download CSV:** Export the list's current values as a `.csv` file.
        
    

[PreviousException Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists)
[NextCreate Promo Code Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-promo-code-exception-lists)

*   [Create the list](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists#create-the-list)
    
*   [Add values to the list](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists#add-values-to-the-list)

# Set Action Locking & Payment Scheduling Periods | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/set-action-locking-and-payment-scheduling-periods.md)
.

A template term's _action locking period_ is the time between when an action is tracked by impact.com and when it locks (or can no longer be updated). During this period, you should perform any necessary action modifications or reversals, since the action can't be updated once this period has passed.

A template term's _payment scheduling period_ is the amount of time between when an action locks and when the partner is paid out for driving the action. During this period, you should ensure that your account has enough funds to cover both the amount recorded on the Statement of Invoices (SOI) and any impact.com invoices.

### Configure your locking period[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/set-action-locking-and-payment-scheduling-periods#configure-your-locking-period)

You can define when an order becomes final and can no longer be reversed by using a fixed _days_ locking period or a dynamic `month + day` locking period.

Days-only locking

Dynamic month-based locking

If you configure your locking period using days only, the maximum value is 90 days.

Use a `Month + Day` configuration when you need a locking period that adjusts dynamically based on the calendar month. Unlike a fixed day count, month-based locking calculates the exact duration relative to the month in which the order occurs.

If you set up a 1-month + 15-day locking period, the platform calculates the total duration by looking at the specific month:

If the month is...

The lock duration will be...

Feb (Standard)

43 Days (28 + 15)

Feb (Leap Year)

44 Days (29 + 15)

Apr, Jun, Sep, Nov

45 Days (30 + 15)

Jan, Mar, May, Jul, Aug, Oct, Dec

46 Days (31 + 15)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] →** [**Settings**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/account-settings-flow.ihtml?execution=e6s1)
    .
    
2.  In the left column, under _General_, select **Template Term Rules**.
    
3.  In the _Rule Enforcement_ section, select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]** below **Application of the rules**.
    
4.  In the _Template Term_ section, ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** **Action Locking** and configure the setting as needed.
    
    *   For example, you can configure this setting as follows: `Actions are locked 0 months and 47 days after the end of the month`.
        
    
5.  Next, ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** **Payment Scheduling** and configure the setting as needed.
    
    *   For example, you can configure this setting as follows: `Actions are paid 0 months and 20 days after the end of the month they lock`.
        
    
6.  Select **Save**.
    

**Example**: If you configured your action locking and payment scheduling periods as shown above, and your partners drove actions during the month of January, all actions will lock on March 19. Come March 20, you'll receive an SOI from impact.com tallying up all partner invoices. At this point, you should begin the process to fund your account sufficiently to cover these invoices _and_ your impact.com invoice. Finally, your account will be debited on April 20 so that your partners can be paid.

[NextStarter Edition for Brands Overview](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/starter-edition-for-brands-overview)
