# Create a Program & Add Users | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/create-a-program-and-add-users.md)
.

A program on impact.com serves as the framework for managing your partnerships, promotions, and campaigns. Follow the steps below to ensure your program is fully set up, accessible to the right users, and ready for success in the marketplace.

**Tip:** If you're considering a new Creator or Advocate program, some Creator or Advocate features may already be included in your current subscription! See [Get Started with Included Creator & Advocate Features](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/get-started-with-included-creator-and-advocate-features)
 instead.

1

#### Step 1: Create a program[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/create-a-program-and-add-users#step-1-create-a-program)

Add a new program to your account drop-down menu in the upper-left corner of your screen.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMTGIl1UdbdrTlM2zaKRR%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-23%2520at%252016.40.33.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7a60f4d1-ede1-44b1-b15e-aa99b033af99&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9f593419c56dff547070fc61885ff4e7&sv=3)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, under _General_, select [**Manage Programs**.](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/accountSettings/view-campaigns-flow.ihtml)
    
3.  Select **Create Program**.
    
4.  Enter a descriptive _Program Name_.
    
5.  Select the **Program Type** you want.
    
    *   See the _Program types reference_ below for more information on each program type.
        
    
6.  Select **Get Started**.
    
    *   You'll be redirected to a screen containing the onboarding checklist for that program.
        
    *   If the program type doesn't support self-serve setup, you may instead see the option to book a meeting with impact.com to discuss the add-on.
        
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FJBfBxiCYn8dxilSUGbZw%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-23%2520at%252016.45.33.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dadd17d99-a99c-40eb-b297-3736dbd0daf0&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7049ebbf7ad28547a50264037c21db7b&sv=3)
    
7.  Complete all the onboarding steps to finish your setup.
    
8.  For Performance programs, you'll need to **Submit For Review** once your setup is complete.
    
    *   This review allows impact.com's internal teams to evaluate your program setup before making you live in the marketplace.
        
    

Program types reference[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/create-a-program-and-add-users#program-types-reference)

Program type

Description

Performance

Partner with affiliate partners who drive clicks, leads, or sales for your brand from their own audiences.

Creator

Collaborate with content creators to produce marketing content and boost audience engagement.

Advocate

Encourage authentic recommendations from satisfied customers to strengthen brand loyalty.

Seller

Drive traffic and sales to your products listed on Amazon through affiliate partners or content creators.

**Important:** When you create a new program, only users in your access group will have access initially. To extend access beyond your group, you’ll need to update any pre-existing access groups as needed.

2

#### Step 2: Add account users[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/create-a-program-and-add-users#step-2-add-account-users)

Once your program is created, you need to grant access to the appropriate account users. This involves setting up custom _User Roles_ and _Access Groups_ for your account.

If you already have these set up, update the relevant access groups to ensure the appropriate users can access the new program. For guidance, see [Manage User Access Groups](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-user-access-groups)
.

1.  Create [Custom Roles](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/create-custom-user-roles)
     based on the types of user permissions you require.
    
2.  Create [User Access Groups](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/create-a-user-access-group)
     to group together users who require the same permissions, and assign each group the appropriate _User Role_.
    
3.  You are now ready to [Invite Account Users](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/invite-account-users)
    .
    
    *   See our article on [Understanding User Management](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/understanding-user-management-as-a-brand)
         for a full overview of the process.
        
    

3

#### Step 3: Make your program productive[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/create-a-program-and-add-users#step-3-make-your-program-productive)

Once the right account users have access to your program, the next step is to complete the rest of your program setup checklist. Required steps include filling out your marketplace profile so partners can learn about your brand, setting up template terms to serve as a contract during signup, and ensuring your technical setup is correctly implemented for conversion tracking.

*   **Complete your marketplace profile** so partners can easily see what your brand is about.
    
*   **Set up template terms** to establish what partners can expect when joining your program.
    
*   **Ensure your event types are correctly set up and tested** so partner-driven conversions appear as expected in your impact.com reporting.
    
*   **Check out our program help doc flows** to get the most value from your program.
    

[PreviousProgram Management](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management)
[NextManage Your Account Programs](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/manage-your-account-programs)

# Create Custom Partner Sign Up Terms for Brands | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/create-custom-partner-sign-up-terms-for-brands.md)
.

You can set standard terms or custom terms for the partner to agree on when they sign up. These are the terms that govern your performance marketing relationship with partners. When setting up your [custom program branding](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding)
, you'll be given the option to select between:

*   Standard Master Program Agreement (MPA)
    
*   Custom Sign Up Terms
    

Standard Master Program Agreement[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/create-custom-partner-sign-up-terms-for-brands#standard-master-program-agreement)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

If you keep this default option, all partner sign-up pages will show impact.com's [Master Program Agreement](https://impact.com/legal/impact.com_Master_Program_Agreement.pdf)
 (MPA).

The Partner Sign Up Terms apply to both new partner sign-ups and existing partners. If you have any questions or concerns about this agreement, [contact support](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml)
.

*   How the terms will display to a partner:
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-e32ca9481c3e07ec61dac574293da1f6efe57f2c%252Fa8ae56401ea874134fa4bf513d10429612414f3173fefd510b63cc426aae3d62.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1189f9400b438f9da4dda3871086bf3d&sv=3)
    

#### Custom Sign Up Terms[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/create-custom-partner-sign-up-terms-for-brands#custom-sign-up-terms)

Customizing your own partner sign up terms allows you to create custom terms to which the partner can agree to when signing up. To set custom sign up terms, do the following:

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the left, under the _Branding_ section, select **Sign Up Page**.
    
3.  In the _Partner Agreement Terms_ section, select **Use my own custom sign up terms (PDF)**.
    
4.  Select **Choose File**.
    
    *   Upload a PDF file of the terms you want the partner to agree on during sign up.
        
    
5.  Select **Save**.
    
    *   How the terms will display to a partner:
        
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-f18c796156993e74b9c7be32a97e1030ed673f86%252Fd0d791072105ba2536d5634a4aa431fb33b855c807c41945154a2d2f4b2515d5.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d2fe5fd91e3aefd959a00f633b9e40d9&sv=3)
    

[PreviousPartner Test Accounts for Brands](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/partner-test-accounts-for-brands)
[NextCreate a Partner Integration](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/beta-create-a-partner-integration)

# Manage Technical Settings | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-technical-settings.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117597?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=tra-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

You can manage advanced settings for your account. If you are unsure about how you want these settings set up, [contact support](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml?createTicket=true)
.

#### Manage technical settings for your account[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-technical-settings#manage-technical-settings-for-your-account)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the left, under _Technical_, select any of the technical settings.
    
3.  Select the setting you want to manage.
    
    **Setting**
    
    **Description**
    
    API
    
    Retrieve your [Impact REST API](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/manage-api-access-tokens-as-a-brand)
     details, like your account SID (Security Identifier).
    
    Data Feeds
    
    Manage your [action feed](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/enable-and-view-data-feeds)
    . You can retrieve reports about new and existing actions linked to your account.
    
    Event Notification
    
    Manage [event notification postbacks](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/notifications/enable-event-notification-postbacks-for-brands)
    . You can synchronize Impact's postback data with a third-party tracker.
    
    Cross Program Tracking
    
    Toggle [cross-program tracking](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/set-up-tracking/enable-cross-program-tracking)
     permissions. This is integral if you have more than one program with impact.com and they are directly interacting with each other, such as a particular partner of yours driving traffic to more than one program at a time.
    
    File Submissions
    
    View recent [FTP file submissions](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/access-ftp-file-submissions)
    . Check the _Status_ column to find if batch submissions were stored successfully.
    
    Partner Custom Values
    
    View all the custom values you've assigned to a partner. Learn how to [assign custom tracking values to partners](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/set-up-tracking/assign-custom-tracking-values-to-partners)
    .
    
    Connections
    
    View all your connections for all batch data file transfers between impact.com and brands, partners, or other parties. Learn more about [connections](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/data-connections-overview)
    .
    
    Tracking Domains
    
    View your custom domains for tracking your programs.
    
    Partner Integrations Hub
    
    Manage and deploy marketing and optimization partner integrations.
    

[PreviousEnable & View Data Feeds](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/enable-and-view-data-feeds)
[NextCustomize Your impact.com Branding](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/customize-your-impactcom-branding)

# Set Third Party Gateway URLs & Click Tracking | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-third-party-gateway-urls-and-click-tracking.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117597?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=tra-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

**Warning:** Before modifying advanced settings discussed in this article, [contact support](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml?createTicket=true)
 or reach out to your CSM.

#### View and modify third-party gateway URL parameters[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-third-party-gateway-urls-and-click-tracking#view-and-modify-third-party-gateway-url-parameters)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the _Program_ column, find _Tracking_, and select **Gateway**.
    
3.  Scroll down to the _Third Party Gateway URL_ field.
    
4.  ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** the _Send all traffic through a third-party gateway URL_ section for further details as seen below.
    
5.  If you want to route traffic through a third-party URL, you can configure the URL, along with any query string parameters you want attached.
    
    *   If you use a third-party tracking system, you can use this feature to record click data in that system.
        
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F8CCpUNch0uFQshL4qccE%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252017.10.20.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df1bef73f-b60f-4107-9395-0e4d1d205f92&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=389df2f3d637b3146f3ba97a6698976a&sv=3)

Query string parameter reference[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-third-party-gateway-urls-and-click-tracking#query-string-parameter-reference)

Parameter

Description

`{gwlurl}`

Your gateway landing page URL to which all visitors will be redirected populated by the `{gwlurl}` token to ensure accurate tracking.

`{clickid}`

The unique click ID assigned to the click event.

`{iradid}`

The referring ad ID.

`{ircid}`

The referring program ID.

`{irpid}`

The referring Partner ID.

`{iradname}`

The referring ad name.

`{iradtype}`

The referring ad type.

`{iradsize}`

The referring ad size (width x height).

`{prodsku}`

The SKU for the product in the referring ad.

`{irmpname}`

The referring partner name.

`{irmptype}`

The referring media type.

`{sharedid}`

The ID provided by the referring partner.

`{irmpgroupname}`

The partner group name(s).

`{mp_value1}`

The Custom Partner Value 1 for the referring partner.

`{mp_value2}`

The Custom Partner Value 2 for the referring partner.

`{mp_value3}`

The Custom Partner Value 3 for the referring partner.

`{timestamp}`

A UNIX timestamp.

`{randint}`

A random integer between 0 and 10,000,000 (excluding 10,000,000).

`{CUSTOM}`

This allows you to capture values that were passed in the tracking link. For instance, if you sent `http://trackinglink.com?myvar=1`, then you could add `{myvar}` to use that in the tracking template.

#### View and modify click tracking settings[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-third-party-gateway-urls-and-click-tracking#view-and-modify-click-tracking-settings)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the _Program Settings_ column, find _Tracking_, and select **Gateway**.
    
3.  In _Click Track Settings_, modify the duration of the click tracking window.
    
    *   The default is the IAB standard 30-minute rolling window, meaning each new click (associated with the same `ClickId`) will keep the click tracking session active.
        
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-f74e84a9506568417d4acf253ad677def93b32af%252F1600418c49f402cc9aa48782d3025831759a913fffc787463f1d800a4ffc777e.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8bb007530e37222d9ca2db9796c19e5b&sv=3)
    

[PreviousGateway Tracking Templates](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates)

# Create Promo Code Exception Lists | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-promo-code-exception-lists.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117596?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=con-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

Promo code exception lists can be used to pay partners different rates based on promo codes associated with actions they drove. You can also create exception lists based on [item categories](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists)
 or [SKUs](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-sku-exception-lists)
.

SKU and promo code [exception lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists)
 are not available in every impact.com edition. For example, Product-Led Growth (PLG) edition accounts do not include the **SKU** or **Promo Code** option under _Exception Lists_. If you do not see either option, you may need to upgrade your account. [Contact support](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/support/customer-support-portal-flow.ihtml)
.

#### Create a promo code exception list[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-promo-code-exception-lists#create-a-promo-code-exception-list)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, under _Exception Lists_, select [**Promo Code**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/campaign/itemlist/view-promocodeitemlist-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  Select **Add New Promo Code Exception List**.
    
4.  Fill out the following information:
    
    Field Name
    
    Description
    
    **List Name**
    
    Add a name for this list—this will only appear internally to you and other account members; partners will not see it.
    
    **Associate Event Type**
    
    Select 1 or more event types to associate with this exception list.
    
    **Match Expression**
    
    **\[Advanced & Optional\]** Enter a regular expression to match specific promo code patterns. Use these articles to configure your regex:
    
    *   [**Regex Explained**](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/promo-codes/regular-expression/regex-explained)
        **:** Understand the basic concepts.
        
    *   [**Regex Inside the Parentheses**](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/promo-codes/regular-expression/regex-inside-the-parentheses)
        **:** Learn how to extract a specific part of a code to match against your list.
        
    *   [**Regex Outside the Parentheses**](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/promo-codes/regular-expression/regex-outside-the-parentheses)
        **:** Learn how to match the full promo code against a pattern.
        
    
5.  Select **Save and Add Items** to continue.
    
6.  You can add a **Promo Code** or you can **Upload a CSV or Excel file** with your promo codes.
    
    *   See the _Promo Code exception list format_ section below for help in building out your .CSV file.
        
    
7.  Select **Save**.
    
    *   You'll now see your new promo code exception list on the [Promo Code Exception Lists screen](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/campaign/itemlist/view-promocodeitemlist-flow.ihtml)
        .
        
    *   If you do not see the **Promo Code** option or this screen, your edition may not include promo code exception lists.
        
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FjvB2MyCWVfNZ3dabgt55%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252017.46.13.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D862a5f46-0c6b-4714-b4bb-ebe7087c9a7c&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a210bd796a2d10118e8e2c91330452a2&sv=3)

Promo Code exception list format[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-promo-code-exception-lists#promo-code-exception-list-format)

Create a .CSV or .XLS/.XLSX file using the column name shown below:

Column

Type

Required

Description

Promo Code

String

Yes

The value that is included in tracking conversion data.

• The values entered here are not case-sensitive.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fg1bsE3RCcxszLwwCvcaU%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252017.50.25.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3fff8576-4efb-4af6-9113-d1c0452c8e5b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f2333fa5b229084183ee87ae8c460113&sv=3)

[PreviousCreate & Manage General Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-and-manage-general-exception-lists)
[NextCreate Category Exception Lists](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/exception-lists/create-category-and-sku-exception-lists)

# Create an API Access Token as a Brand | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/create-an-api-access-token-as-a-brand.md)
.

_Account Administrators_ and _Implementation Specialist_ members of a brand account can enable impact.com API access and view account credentials. Follow the steps below to create a new API token and configure access scopes for the token. Read some tips on how to [keep your access tokens secure](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/api-access-tokens-and-changelog/keep-your-access-tokens-secure)
.

**Note:** Visit impact.com's [Integrations Portal](https://integrations.impact.com/brand-api-reference)
 to view API reference documentation, integration guides, and more.

#### Create an access token[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/create-an-api-access-token-as-a-brand#create-an-access-token)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\]** → **Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, scroll to _Technical_, then select **API**.
    
3.  Create a new token by selecting **Create Access Token** on the upper-right side of the page.
    

#### Create a token[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/create-an-api-access-token-as-a-brand#create-a-token)

1\. Setup[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/create-an-api-access-token-as-a-brand#id-1.-setup)

*   Enter a suitable name into the **Token Name** text field.
    
*   Add a **Description** that describes the purpose of the token for your reference in the future.
    
*   Specify the **API Version** that the token will be compatible with. We suggest using the latest API version, which is also the default choice.
    
*   Select **Next**.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FAluBSdXhBLmUbLDwLexN%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-20%2520at%252011.04.17.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dbd378028-d5b8-4f2b-9eb1-699d4ae792c7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=019ddef307638d27008041e4c71296af&sv=3)

2\. Contact Information[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/create-an-api-access-token-as-a-brand#id-2.-contact-information)

*   Select a **Primary Contact** from the drop-down menu. We'll be in touch with the primary contact about development updates.
    
*   Optionally, add email addresses for developers who should know about updates related to the access token. This is handy when creating tokens for developers who are not account users.
    
*   Select **Next**.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fek8rS9xyjkynutZrdw9V%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-20%2520at%252011.07.07.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1215cadf-8474-4ae8-8faf-c592411314a7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7ac3ad2f00e1fe80a29a4477a33a6b6a&sv=3)

3\. API Scopes[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/create-an-api-access-token-as-a-brand#id-3.-api-scopes)

*   Toggle an API category on, then check the checkboxes on the left to select the access scopes that you would like to allow on your API token.
    
*   You can clear all scopes by clicking the **Clear All** button near the top-right of the page.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FesmY9AcxNd2InyjDv3OK%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-20%2520at%252011.11.23.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db077402a-aafc-457b-9426-210bdd622a41&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e2719edaf652596eca7550ed1e979027&sv=3)

4\. Select Create[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/create-an-api-access-token-as-a-brand#id-4.-select-create)

*   Finally, select **Create** on the lower right of the page.
    

[PreviousAPI Tokens](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens)
[NextEdit API Access Tokens as a Brand](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/api-tokens/edit-api-access-tokens-as-a-brand)

# Data Connections Overview | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/data-connections-overview.md)
.

_Connectors_ are a system for all batch data file transfers between impact.com and brands, partners, or other parties. Each connection (an individual connector) has certain attributes, such as the data type (clicks, conversions, product catalogs, etc.), direction (inbound or outbound), method (SFTP, FTP, Email), and more.

#### View the Connections screen[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/data-connections-overview#view-the-connections-screen)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, under _Technical_, select **Connections**.
    
    *   The _Connections_ screen is where you can create new connections. It shows existing connections, statuses, and actions.
        
    

Columns & filters[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/data-connections-overview#columns-and-filters)

**Program**

Select the specific program whose connections you want to view or edit.

**Type**

Refers to the type of data this connection processes (e.g., conversion data, action modifications, etc.)

**Direction**

Refers to the direction of the data that this connection supports: _Inbound_ is data coming into impact.com, _Outbound_ is data going out of impact.com to another platform.

**Method**

Refers to the transfer method this connection uses: _FTP, SFTP, or Email_ are currently supported.

**Encryption**

Shows the encryption key pair, if available. Encryption is only available for inbound connections (uploads to impact.com), not outbound (downloads from impact.com).

**Status /Last Processing Status**

Refers to the latest status for the connection:

• None

• In Progress — data transfer via this connection is currently in progress.

• Completed — data transfer via this connection has recently been completed.

• Failed — data transfer via this connection has recently failed; view the connection history for details.

**Name**

Given name of an existing connection.

**Last Active**

Refers to the last time this connection's status was updated.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-1e0bbc2fbb260c9846c479063dc2a10f301c5a3b%252Ffb08358c10eb18ae39cd472335b74bd43ce45f7c7e5ff705cd708d9d2b84d37d.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f33df08eeeec222cab108a0badb9bd6f&sv=3) **\[More\]**

Hover over a connection to see this option. Here, you can review the actions you can take for this connection: _Edit_ or _View History_ to see the status history for this connection.

#### Learn more[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/data-connections-overview#learn-more)

*   [Configure a Data Download Connection](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection)
    
*   [Configure a Data Upload Connection](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection)
    
*   [Review Data Connection Submissions](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/review-data-connection-submissions)
    

[PreviousSend Data to impact.com](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom)
[NextCreate a Data Upload Connection](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection)

# Create a Notification for Monthly Volume Limits | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/notifications/create-a-notification-for-monthly-volume-limits.md)
.

If you're on a monthly payment processing contract with impact.com, you can easily configure a custom notification to alert you (or any account members) when your account is approaching your volume limit.

*   This notification only works for **monthly payment processing volume contracts**, not annual contracts. If you're on an annual processing volume contract and want to know where you are based on your annual limit, contact us via support or ask your CSM.
    
*   The notification is based on non-locked action volume, meaning the actual processing volume amount is subject to change based on reversals, contract changes, etc. It should be used as an estimate, not as the final amount.
    

#### Configure a volume limit notification[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/notifications/create-a-notification-for-monthly-volume-limits#configure-a-volume-limit-notification)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-07f24e024450a8d837d6e6c7e086a7b6141a0d2b%252F18ef9fdffe47daac8e10e43d89cb1ee751acfe5dd798aa924275b5c5e62cb043.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=dfa76fa56f4f8a6ba922aebf01f31e0d&sv=3) **\[Notifications\]**.
    
2.  In the upper right corner of the notification pane, select **Settings** .
    
3.  At the top of the screen, select the **Custom Notifications** tab.
    
4.  Hover over a notification you want to edit and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **Edit**.
    
5.  In the _Notification Details_ section, select the **Account** for this notification from the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\].**
    
6.  Add a **Notification Name** and add a **Notification Description**. The description will appear in the notification that's sent.
    
7.  In the _Notification Rules_ section, use the _Look at activity by_ ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** to select **Media across all programs**.
    
8.  For _Run Rules_, select **Once a Day**.
    
9.  For the _Date Range_, select **Month to Date**.
    
10.  In the _Rules_ box, use the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** to create the following logic:
    
    `[Total cost]` `[greater than or equals]` `[monthly volume processing limit]`.
    
    **Note:** Refer to your contract with impact.com to find your monthly payout processing volume amount.
    
11.  Choose whether or not to share this with other members of your impact.com account, then select **Save**.

# Creator Edit Configuration | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)

# Manage 2FA for Brand Accounts | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-2fa-for-brand-accounts.md)
.

[Account Administrators](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/understanding-user-management-as-a-brand)
 can require all impact.com brand account members to use two-factor authentication (2FA) when they sign in from a new device. Have questions about 2FA? Check our article on [Two-Factor Authentication FAQs](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/two-factor-authentication-faqs)
.

When invited to join an account and your sign-up method involves using a username and password, you'll be automatically signed up for 2FA and your default authentication method will be set to email.

The user experience with 2FA enabled goes as follows:

*   Upon first signing in, an account member will receive a verification code on their mobile device via SMS, email, or an authentication app.
    
*   Once an account member has successfully been authenticated, their device will automatically be saved as a trusted device—meaning they won’t need to use 2FA on subsequent sign-ins. Account members will only be asked to use 2FA again if their device has changed or unusual activity was detected.
    
*   Account members can manage saved devices on the impact.com user profile screen.
    

#### Turn on 2FA for sign-in[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-2fa-for-brand-accounts#turn-on-2fa-for-sign-in)

This will enable mandatory two-factor authentication for all account members who sign in to your brand account.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, under _General_, select **Account User Authentication**.
    
3.  On the _Account user Authentication_ screen ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\] User Login**.
    
4.  Select **Save**.
    
    *   View the users who have activated 2FA, on the **Account Users** screen, indicated by a tick in the _Two-Factor Authentication_ column.
        
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-9150fe247bb2b9e4eef99a692f70b4224a4165d6%252Fb86d031317647e13314fc45ded07828b005240b4fe9bdf915bf5343fc24a5dd3.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=fd6fa18b3b53c038e6e9dd406ed9d65f&sv=3)
    

#### Turn on 2FA for PFTs[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-2fa-for-brand-accounts#turn-on-2fa-for-pfts)

This will enable a mandatory 2FA check for account users attempting a [partner funds transfer](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/partner-funds-transfer/transfer-funds-to-a-partner)
 above the transfer minimum amount — once authenticated, the PFT will go through.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, under _General_, select **Account User Authentication**.
    
3.  On the _Account User Authentication_ screen, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\] Partner Funds Transfer**.
    
4.  Under _Transfer minimum_, **input a value** as the minimum amount to require 2FA.
    
5.  Select **Save**.
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-00bddccf23fcfb1db59647fc66de60c14637d8ab%252Ffbdcb65c97b79d5d1eb1a1eeb3eefed5af074f35c03459c0b43500cb66cf7f63.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=166d0ca546552fe6ad7ed6ef38a64f2a&sv=3)
    

#### Manage saved 2FA devices[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-2fa-for-brand-accounts#manage-saved-2fa-devices)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User Profile\] → Edit Profile**.
    
2.  Go to the _Security_ section.
    
3.  Under _Devices and Activity_, there is a list of saved devices for the account and the date and time of the last login from that device.
    
4.  Select **Remove this device** if you no longer access impact.com on that device.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FbjCPx9FxjrZBFhnh4cvR%252FUntitled%2520design.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D58bc8895-ddad-4af6-8d8f-84eb066e9180&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f23b1208bcd933829eddc2890c91671d&sv=3)

# Grow Your Partnerships with Essentials Edition | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/grow-your-partnerships-with-essentials-edition.md)
.

[Upgrade Subscription](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/finance/fr/finance-subscription.ihtml?u=%2Fsecure%2Ffinance%2Fapp%2Fview-subscription-flow.ihtml)

When you upgrade from Starter Edition to Essentials Edition, you’ll get more features like advanced partner discovery, greater tracking flexibility, more contract customization, robust workflow tools, and seamless services to elevate your team.

Our Essentials Edition gives you extra horsepower to fast-track your partnerships program from fledgling to mature.

*   **Get unlimited access to impact.com’s partner marketplace**
    
    Access 110,000+ partners across affiliates, influencers, premium publishers, and more, and recruit them directly to your partnerships program.
    
*   **Go beyond online conversions – track any event you want**
    
    Essentials Edition allows you to track any event whether it’s leads, installs, offline conversions, or bookings.
    
*   **Customize contracts to your needs, without limitations**
    
    Configure performance bonuses, cost-per-click payouts, and advanced payout rules to optimize results.
    

#### Unlock more features[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/grow-your-partnerships-with-essentials-edition#unlock-more-features)

Check out this feature-by-feature comparison of the Starter and Essentials editions to identify key differences between the two tiers.

Features

Starter Edition

Essentials Edition

Pricing

Starts at $30/mo

Starts at $500/mo

[Access to Discover Marketplace](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/performance-program/recruiting-partners/discover/discover-overview)

**No**

**Yes**

[Application Workflow Automation](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/review-partner-applications/automate-partner-application-processing/create-workflows-to-automatically-process-partner-applications)

**Limited**

**Yes**

Access to Agency Partners

**No**

**Yes**

[CPC Payouts](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/set-up-tracking/cost-per-click-v1-and-pending-clicks-explained)

**No**

**Yes**

[Payout Groups](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/contracts-and-template-terms/template-terms/payout-adjustments/set-payout-rules-using-payout-groups)

**Limited**

**Yes**

[Performance Bonuses](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/contracts-and-template-terms/template-terms/payout-adjustments/performance-bonus/add-a-performance-bonus-to-a-contract)

**No**

**Yes**

[Partner Funds Transfers](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/partner-funds-transfer/transfer-funds-to-a-partner)

**Yes**

[Chained Events](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/actions-and-payouts/actions/track-your-conversion-funnel-using-chained-actions)

**No**

**Yes**

[Data Lab](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/data-lab-introduction)

**No**

**Yes**

Want to learn how impact.com can help your brand grow? [Request a demo](https://impact.com/request-a-demo-for-brands/)
 to learn more.

#### Upgrade to Essentials[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/grow-your-partnerships-with-essentials-edition#upgrade-to-essentials)

If you'd like to upgrade from starter to essentials edition:

1.  From the top navigation bar, select **your balance \[Drop-down menu\] → Subscription**.
    
2.  Select **Upgrade Subscription** at the bottom of the _Subscription_ page. Alternatively, select **Upgrade Subscription** at the start of this article.
    
    *   This button can also be found on your _My Partners_ page or on the dashboard homepage.
        
    
3.  Then select **Choose Plan** under the _Performance Essentials_ tile.
    
    *   You can also select **Book Demo** in the top right corner if you want to see a demo of the product first, or select **Contact Us** in the top right corner to book a session with an impact.com representative to discuss the program types first.
        
    
4.  Once you select the plan, you'll be asked to review your subscription choice. Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Checkbox\]** **Performance Essentials** under the _Authorization_ section once ready and then select **Accept New Subscription** to confirm the upgrade.
    
5.  You should then see an interstitial window to confirm your request has been submitted and that the upgrade is in progress. You'll be required to log out of the account and log back in, in order to see your new upgraded account.
    

[PreviousStarter Edition for Brands Overview](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/starter-edition-for-brands-overview)

# Customize Your Program Branding | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117599?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=eng-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

If you're managing multiple programs, you can set custom branding, manage the sign-up page, retrieve your partner sign-up link, and set some automated emails and messages associated with a specific program. You can also manage the [branding for your entire account](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/customize-your-impactcom-branding)
.

#### Toggle between programs[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#toggle-between-programs)

Before you manage your program branding, ensure that the correct program is selected.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  Next to _Program_, from the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**, select the program for which you want to manage the branding.
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FM5ZmjhmAZc0ixr7HMTvS%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-23%2520at%252015.49.23.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D4bb53c7d-3920-4896-aa97-935d3cefbb49&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0c76b691794a26ef6ba10f1242b496dd&sv=3)
    

#### Manage the partner sign-up page[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#manage-the-partner-sign-up-page)

One of the first pages your direct partners will see is the sign-up page. You can manage the styling of this page to suit your brand identity.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, under _Branding_, select **Partner Sign Up Page**.
    
3.  [Customize your partner sign-up page](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/performance-program/recruiting-partners/customize-the-partner-sign-up-page)
     as desired.
    
4.  Apply the changes and select **Save**.
    

#### System Emails and UI Messages[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#system-emails-and-ui-messages)

Manage what the automated messages (e.g., welcome messages or rejection messages) associated with this program will say. These messages are unique to each program and need to be set up for each one.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, under _Branding_, select **System Emails and UI Messages**.
    
3.  Next to the setting you want to change, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fkka3dqJOx8ZhiX82RDLt%252FEdit_light.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df454405c-ebd8-49af-90db-22efc27476bf&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9628ee367a677342e943e90378c7c017&sv=3) **\[Edit\]**.
    
    *   See the sections below for a breakdown of these settings.
        
    
4.  Apply the changes and select **Save**.
    

Email Branding[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#email-branding)

**Setting**

**Description**

Branded Email Settings

Select if you want to use [account branding settings](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/customize-your-impactcom-branding)
 or program-specific branding settings. For program-specific settings, use the color-pickers and fields to customize your emails.

Preview Branded Email

Select **Open in new window** to see what the automated email for your program will look like.

Welcome Message[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#welcome-message)

**Setting**

**Description**

Welcome Email

Select if you want to send an automated email to welcome partners after a partnership has been initiated.

Select if you want to support multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter email copy.

Select **Preview** to see what your welcome email will look like.

Welcome UI Message

Select if you want to send an automated welcome UI message once a partner has logged in.

Select if you want to support multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter message copy.

Enter a title and HTML code for the message copy.

Sign Up Message[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#sign-up-message)

**Setting**

**Description**

Sign Up Email

Select if you want to send an automated email to acknowledge a partner's signup.

Select if you want to support multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter email copy.

Enter the email subject and the HTML code for the message copy.

Select **Preview** to see what your signup email will look like.

Sign Up UI Message

Select if you want to send an automated UI message to welcome partners after they log in.

Select if you want to support multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter message copy.

Enter a title and HTML code for the message copy.

Rejection Message[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#rejection-message)

**Setting**

**Description**

Rejection Email

Select if you want to send impact.com's default rejection email or a program-specific rejection email.

For the program-specific email, select if you want to support multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter email copy.

Enter a subject and HTML code for the email copy.

Select **Preview** to see what your rejection email will look like.

Rejection UI Message

Select if you want to use impact.com's default rejection UI message or a program-specific rejection UI message.

For the program-specific message, select if you want to select multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter message copy.

Enter a title and HTML code for the message copy.

Suspension Message[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/customize-your-program-branding#suspension-message)

**Setting**

**Description**

Suspension Email

Select if you want to send impact.com's default suspension email or a program-specific rejection email.

For the program-specific email, select if you want to support multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter email copy.

Enter a subject and HTML code for the email copy.

Select **Preview** to see what your suspension email will look like.

Suspension UI Message

Select if you want to use impact.com's default suspension UI message or a program-specific rejection UI message.

For the program-specific message, select if you want to select multiple languages. If you do, select for which language you want to enter message copy.

Enter a title and HTML code for the message copy.

# Access FTP File Submissions | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/access-ftp-file-submissions.md)
.

You can access your FTP file submissions to check the status of recent FTP file submissions for batches of reversals, modifications, or new conversions. You'll be able to separate failed and successful records, allowing you to diagnose potential issues on an individual record level. Files will only be stored and accessible for 90 days after initial submission.

#### Access your FTP file submissions[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/access-ftp-file-submissions#access-your-ftp-file-submissions)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the left, under _Technical_, select **File Submissions**.
    
3.  On the _FTP File Submissions_ screen, use the filters to adjust what data is displayed.
    
    *   See the _Filter reference_ for more information.
        
    *   The FTP file submission results displayed in the table provide you with more insight into your file submissions, including the total number of records added to the file, any errors that occurred, and the date and time the file was submitted.
        
    

Filter reference[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/access-ftp-file-submissions#filter-reference)

Filter

Description

Search box

Search for the FTP file submission using the file name or ID.

Type

Filter for the file submission type:

• **Conversions:** [Conversion data](https://integrations.impact.com/brand-api-reference/reference/action-and-conversion-field-references)
 (i.e., actions).

• **Modifications:** [Reversals or modifications](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/submit-and-modify-conversion-data/batch-modify-conversion-data/submit-batch-modifications-and-reversals)
 when something about an action has changed, like an order gets canceled.

• **Catalog:** A [product catalog data feed](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/product-catalogs/add-product-catalogs-as-a-brand)
 that holds a list of your products and their metadata.

Status

The current status of the FTP file submission.

Data Source

Filter by the source from which the file was submitted.

#### Download the original batch file[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/access-ftp-file-submissions#download-the-original-batch-file)

1.  On the _FTP File Submissions_ screen, hover over the submission for which you want to download the file.
    
2.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **Download Original File**.
    
    *   The .CSV file will be downloaded to your computer.
        
    

#### View FTP file submission errors[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/access-ftp-file-submissions#view-ftp-file-submission-errors)

You can view file submission errors on the _FTP_ [_File Submissions_](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/accountSettings/techintegration/ftp-file-submissions-flow.ihtml)
 screen.

1.  On the _FTP File Submissions_ screen, hover over the submission for which you want to view the errors.
    
2.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **View Errors**.
    
3.  Use the **Error Type** filter to filter the errors by **Validation** or **Contract Error**.
    
    *   To better understand the error code found in the _Message_ column, please refer to the [impact.com Error Codes Reference](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/impactcom-error-codes-reference)
         article.
        
    

[PreviousImport Aggregate Performance Data from Other Networks](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/import-aggregate-performance-data-from-other-networks)

# Partner Test Accounts for Brands | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/partner-test-accounts-for-brands.md)
.

As a brand, you may sometimes want access to the partner user interface. This can give you a better understanding of the partner experience and help you answer questions partners may have. To access the partner platform, you'll need to create your own partner test account. While this test account won't have access to the Marketplace, it can help you preview things from the perspective of your partners.

Use cases for a partner test account[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/partner-test-accounts-for-brands#use-cases-for-a-partner-test-account)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   To have a better understanding of the partner UI and signup experience
    
*   To test your own links, ads, and to create test orders
    
*   To receive your own newsletters and view what partners see
    
*   To receive partner-facing release notes from impact.com and keep up to date with partner-side changes.
    

**Note:** The steps below demonstrate how to create a partner account without applying to the Brands Marketplace. The process links your test account directly to your program. Other brands cannot view the account, nor will you have access to the Marketplace.

1

**Create a partner test account**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/partner-test-accounts-for-brands#create-a-partner-test-account)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\] → Contracts →** [**Template Terms**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/engage/contracts/library/view-manage-ios-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
2.  Hover over the terms to which you want your test account signed up, at the end of the row, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-1e0bbc2fbb260c9846c479063dc2a10f301c5a3b%252Ffb08358c10eb18ae39cd472335b74bd43ce45f7c7e5ff705cd708d9d2b84d37d.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f33df08eeeec222cab108a0badb9bd6f&sv=3) **\[More\] →** **Get Branded Links**.
    
3.  Open your sign-up link.
    
    *   You will be taken to the partner-facing sign-up page for your program.
        
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-1161ad227f340ec9827d8df9c79f172a8d46d910%252F3311963ffa92225ae0a960a39404c1c2134a6fa61202d5f8c1d5230bd746d542.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e6dccf04be43c82d62f3ea000bde78e7&sv=3)
    
4.  Select **Sign up**.
    
5.  Your own program terms will be presented for review. **Accept** the terms.
    
6.  You will be requested to either log in or create a partner account. Select **Sign up with email**.
    
7.  Fill in the fields as prompted, using an email address and a username that is not associated with an existing impact.com account.
    
8.  Select **Get started**.
    
9.  On the pages that follow, enter the same _personal_, _company_, and _promotional information_ as your brand account.
    
    *   For more assistance with the signup process, see [Sign Up as a Partner on impact.com](https://help.impact.com/partner/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/getting-started/sign-up-as-a-partner-on-impactcom)
        .
        
    
10.  Once you have completed the process, you will receive an email from impact.com confirming the creation of your user profile. Follow the prompts to activate your profile and create a new password.
    

2

**Approve your test account**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/partner-test-accounts-for-brands#approve-your-test-account)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Log in to your brand account.
    
2.  From your left navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\] → Partners →** [**Applications**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/engage/contracts/activity/adv-manage-pending-public-ios-v2-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  Locate your own program application, and at the end of the row, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\] → Accept**.
    

3

**Toggle between your brand and test accounts**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/partner-test-accounts-for-brands#toggle-between-your-brand-and-test-accounts)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

After creating a test partner account, you can [link it with your brand account](https://help.impact.com/partner/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-management/account-settings/how-can-i-merge-my-impactcom-partner-accounts)
 to allow you to easily switch between accounts.

1.  Log in to your brand account.
    
2.  Select your program name in the upper-left corner to expand the dropdown and view your other accounts.
    
    *   From here, you can toggle between your brand and partner test accounts.
        
    

[PreviousManage Tasks](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks)
[NextCreate Custom Partner Sign Up Terms for Brands](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/create-custom-partner-sign-up-terms-for-brands)
