# Advocate Team Member Roles & Permissions | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/advocate-team-member-roles-and-permissions.md)
.

Multiple members of your team can have access to your Advocate program’s settings. These team members may be assigned one of four roles:

*   Full access
    
*   Program manager
    
*   Support
    
*   Viewer
    

impact.com recommends regularly auditing your list of team members to ensure that only current employees have the right level of access.

**Note:** You need to [set up a user access group](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/manage-user-access-groups)
 with Advocate access, which is where you will select these roles.

Full access

Program manager

Support

Viewer

Full access team members can view and edit all elements of your Advocate program’s content and setup details. Only people with the full access role can invite other team members to your account or edit the permissions of existing team members. They can’t change their own permissions or remove themselves.

impact.com recommends reserving full access permissions for those who need access to integrations and microsite hosting settings.

Permissions[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/advocate-team-member-roles-and-permissions#permissions)

*   Manage team
    
*   Billing
    
*   Manage microsite hosting and identity
    
*   Integration management
    
*   Manage API keys
    
*   View programs
    
*   Manage programs
    
*   Manage forms
    
*   View participants
    
*   Manage participants
    
*   Review and manage [pending referrals](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/advocate-program/manage-advocate-participants/referrals-and-events/review-pending-referrals-for-advocate)
    
*   Data management
    
*   Reports and analytics
    

Program managers can create and manage your company’s programs, rewards, data, analytics, and participant information. They do not have the ability to set up or modify microsites and integrations.

impact.com recommends the program manager role for high-touch users who need to frequently interact with our platform without making high-level decisions related to integrations and account settings.

Permissions[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/advocate-team-member-roles-and-permissions#permissions-1)

*   View programs
    
*   Manage programs
    
*   Manage forms
    
*   View participants
    
*   Manage participants
    
*   Review and manage [pending referrals](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/advocate-program/manage-advocate-participants/referrals-and-events/review-pending-referrals-for-advocate)
    
*   Data management
    
*   Reports and analytics
    

Team members with the support role have some view permissions and access to the areas of our platform that are more important for troubleshooting. They can view program details, data, and analytics, but they can only modify (not create) participant details and information.

impact.com recommends the support role for anyone involved with troubleshooting how user and event data are transmitted to impact.com.

Permissions[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/advocate-team-member-roles-and-permissions#permissions-2)

*   View programs
    
*   View participants
    
*   Manage participants
    
*   Reports and analytics
    

Viewers have the most limited set of permissions. They’re unable to make changes to your programs or run reports of their own. Instead, they can view programs, data, and analytics, as well as details for individual program participants. They can also export existing reports.

The viewer role is ideal for someone who needs visibility into your Advocate program and its performance without the ability to modify setup or content.

Permissions[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/advocate-team-member-roles-and-permissions#permissions-3)

*   View programs
    
*   View participants
    
*   Reports and analytics

# Configure a Data Download Connection | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection.md)
.

Connectors are a system for all batch data file transfers between impact.com and brands, partners, or other parties. Each connection (an individual connector) has certain attributes, such as the data type (clicks, conversions, product catalogs, etc.), direction (inbound or outbound), method (SFTP, FTP, Email), and more.

The Connector Wizard allows you to set up connections to other platforms so that impact.com can automatically batch process the data to deliver enriched data to your systems.

#### Create an outbound connection[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#create-an-outbound-connection)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, under _Technical_, select **Connections**.
    
3.  Select **Create Connection** in the top right to access the Connector wizard.
    
    *   You can't delete a connection once it's been created.
        
    

Connector terminology[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#connector-terminology)

Term

Description

Connection Name

Provide a name that describes the connection. (E.g., Download Aggregate Performance Data.).

Direction

The direction of the data that this connection supports: Inbound is data coming into impact.com, Outbound is data going out of impact.com to another platform.

Data Type

The type of data this connection processes (E.g., conversion data, action modifications, etc.).

Method

The transfer method this connection uses, (E.g., FTP, SFTP, or Email.).

Methodology

The transfer methodology. Inbound connections support “Push to Impact” and “Pull from 3rd Party”. Outbound connections support “Pull from Impact”.

Connection Details

The connection details that will be required is dependant on a combination of the chosen Direction, Method and Methodology.

In some cases the you will need to provide details and in other cases impact.com will provide the details.

**Note:** Read more about how to [Give data to impact.com](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection)
.

#### Receive data from impact.com[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#receive-data-from-impact.com)

1

#### **Step 1: Direction**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#step-1-direction)

1.  Enter the connection name and select the _Direction_ of the data.
    
2.  Select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-590bdf4e1ffbcc1fe86ad456bebab6a28c64af1f%252Ffd6e4291628446bb1fe27a831f7874b256a28cc84833d72cce2e73b94641edb7.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c0ae24100ce670ad15e57d05fb25b1b1&sv=3) **\[Radio Button\]** to **Receive data from impact.com**.
    
3.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-f574f24a4d3307db3d03b524cf8c63055a7afba5%252Fe1ae2f5ee0c1e8e8f49277864b82f8bd9846f6282093449100cbb4f289f27826.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=358d67a37cec9f41eb9c394920131149&sv=3)

2

#### **Step 2: Type**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#step-2-type)

1.  Select the type of data that the connection will support: **Performance Bonus Detail** or **Reports**.
    
2.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FqlHDYmIIJixm9TNAoFtK%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-16%2520at%252018.12.20%2520%282%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dec744ad4-b5e0-4042-ab75-acb622442d40&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=42884deafdcd66e9fb9d469f1537739a&sv=3)

3

#### **Step 3: Method**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#step-3-method)

1.  The _Method_ will be selected for you: **SFTP**. You can change this if needed.
    
2.  The _Methodology_ will be selected for you: **Pull from impact.com**.
    
3.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FdeFyarL91Rr2RyQikg5p%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-16%2520at%252018.14.42.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De871bfce-a473-4106-a448-ad2f93497bfd&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9dc59a3de968b9fcafd4f60fc5db6da9&sv=3)

4

#### **Step 4: Credentials**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#step-4-credentials)

1.  Either select **existing credentials** from the dropdown, **Edit Existing Credentials** or **Create New Credentials**.
    
    *   If you're creating new credentials, enter a name for the credentials that describes its use case. E.g., _Download aggregate performance data_, then select either **Public Key → Create** or **Password → Create**.
        
    *   If you choose **Password**, the values will be populated for you. Be sure to **Copy** these values.
        
    
2.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FYSaVrnF2HMgoz1Zh3zLP%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-17%2520at%252009.25.22.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1857df11-108e-46bb-8de3-b75c1e19a17f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=89c438a28d5d412186a3722edd5922d2&sv=3)

5

#### **Step 5: Review**[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection#step-5-review)

1.  Finally, review the configuration and then select **Create** to create the connection.
    

[PreviousCreate a Data Upload Connection](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection)
[NextReview Data Connection Submissions](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/review-data-connection-submissions)

# Download a Report | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/download-a-report.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117602?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=opt-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

You can share the reports that impact.com generates with other stakeholders without them needing to log in to the impact.com platform themselves. To do this, you'll need to download the reports from impact.com as CSV, Excel, or PDF files. The report will always be downloaded as a table, and some will come with a graph as well.

#### Download a report[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/download-a-report#download-a-report)

1.  From the left navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\] → Reports**, then select the report you want to export.
    
    *   Learn more about [managing reports](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/manage-reports)
        .
        
    
2.  In the upper-right corner, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-1c990ed7d1179d6bc54ec8a2f98688d63ffdf977%252F1ca7fd10d94ed494c076e86be8f0d3f371693b11c47f1b26ec0435a27e0e48c5.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=43d1fa950550d68b842cbeb73b475611&sv=3) **\[Download\]**.
    
3.  From the drop-down menu, select what file type you want the report in.
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FIBMMG587Zgcgfy0a2QhB%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-27%2520at%252016.40.00.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2669f796-3b0e-4180-b3a8-882bc55ee688&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f4b62544a43e1f850f2bacaa51567d38&sv=3)
    

**Note:** The tail end of very large data sets may not be included in your downloaded file.

[PreviousMore Reports Screen](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/more-reports-screen)
[NextSchedule Reports](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/schedule-reports)

# Pin a Report | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/pin-a-report.md)
.

You can pin any report you find on the **More Reports** screen to the left navigation menu. Pinned reports will appear in a drop-down menu that is labeled by the kind of report it is. For example, a pinned _Performance_ report will appear within the _Performance_ drop-down menu. Most drop-down menus (e.g., _Operations_) will not appear in the left navigation menu until after you have pinned a report of that type.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Reports → More Reports**.
    
2.  Find the report you want to pin to your left navigation menu.
    
    *   Use the filters at the top of the screen to assist your search.
        
    
3.  Hover your cursor over the report and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FKG07S1laT6SIusRgHwBA%252FPin.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1fef79ba-7abe-4eb4-8341-5b33074bee1c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5866b1ed652edf35cefe19185adb2a94&sv=3) **\[Pin\]** to pin the report.
    

[PreviousManage Reports](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/manage-reports)
[NextSet a Customized Reporting Calendar](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/set-a-customized-reporting-calendar)

# Schedule a Custom Report | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/schedule-a-custom-report.md)
.

The following steps will guide you through scheduling a custom report for a specific date or regular intervals:

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Reports → More Reports**.
    
2.  Select **Category** and filter for **Built** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]**.
    
3.  Hover over the _Custom_ report you want to schedule and select **View**.
    
4.  In the top-right corner, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-20b2988bdc1f8a97da2944a414cd37208d6827a3%252Fb90978d4ad1499f25650dc65b2602f870a0db03c6b9b1a5b49af856b768d2cbe.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=763e4e27e20a716b0902168faaac7edd&sv=3) **\[Schedule\]**.
    
5.  Set how frequently the report should be sent using the _Frequency_ section.
    
    *   Custom reports can be sent **Daily, Weekly**, or **Monthly**.
        
    
6.  Select what **Format** in which the report should be sent.
    
    *   Custom reports can be sent as a PDF, CSV _with_ Meta Data, or CSV _without_ Meta Data.
        
        **Note:** When opting to schedule a CSV without metadata, the downloaded ZIP file will not have the `metadata.json` file included.
        
    
7.  Pick whether the report should be delivered via **Email** or **Partner FTP**.
    
    *   Specify if impact.com should still **Send if report has no data**.
        
    
8.  Enter the requested information for the delivery method you selected in Step 5.
    
    *   Learn more about delivery types from the Delivery method reference section of the [Scheduled Reports](https://help.impact.com/partner/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reporting-for-partners/report-management/schedule-a-report-as-a-partner)
         article.
        
    
9.  Select **Schedule**.
    

[PreviousEmail a Custom Report](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/email-a-custom-report)
[NextDownload a Custom Report](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/download-a-custom-report)

# Request a New Program | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program.md)
.

**Important:** This process applies to new **Performance** programs.

Follow these steps when you need impact.com to add a new Performance program to your account. The checklist collects the setup details we need to configure your program.

*   If you have permission to create programs directly, see [_Create a Program & Add Users_](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/create-a-program-and-add-users)
     instead.
    
*   If you want to add Creator or Advocate functionality, see [_Get Started with Included Creator & Advocate Features_](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/get-started-with-included-creator-and-advocate-features)
    .
    
*   If you're setting up a program to sell on Amazon, see [_Amazon Seller Program Explained_](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/seller-program/amazon-seller-program/amazon-seller-program-explained)
     for the dedicated Amazon integration setup flow.
    

Submit your program request[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program#submit-your-program-request)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the left, under _General_, select [**Manage Programs**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/tracking-settings/actiontracker/view-actiontracker-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  In the upper-right corner, select **Create Program**.
    
4.  Complete the information in the following main sections:
    

Overview[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program#overview)

Provide high-level details about the new program you want to create. This includes basic setup, funding, contacts, and technical information.

1.  **Program Information**
    
    *   Indicate the number of [new programs](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/create-a-program-and-add-users#program-types-reference)
         you want to add to your account.
        
    *   Provide a name for the new program.
        
    
2.  **Funding**
    
    *   Confirm whether you’ll [fund](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/add-funds-to-your-funding-account/guide-to-funding-strategies-for-brands)
         the new program in the same currency as your existing programs. The program will be invoiced in your chosen currency, and this can’t be changed once the account is active.
        
    *   Choose your invoicing option — a separate invoice for the new program, or a combined invoice listing the charges for each program individually.
        
    *   Since launching a new program may affect your contract, indicate if you’d like to review your agreement with an impact.com representative before going live.
        
    
3.  **Contacts**
    
    *   Confirm if your current team will manage the new program.
        
    *   Provide the names and emails of your technical contacts assigned to this project.
        
    
4.  **Technical Details**
    
    *   Enter the website domains and subdomains associated with the new program.
        
    *   Enter the default landing page.
        
    *   Select the option that best describes what [event types](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/event-types/event-types-explained)
         you want to track.
        
    

Mobile Events (Optional)[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program#mobile-events-optional)

Complete this section only if your new program includes [mobile app tracking](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/mobile-app-tracking/mobile-app-tracking-explained)
. Provide the following details for app conversions to be tracked accurately.

1.  **General Details**
    
    *   Indicate which platforms support the mobile app, e.g., iOS or Android.
        
    *   Provide the names of the apps for which you want to track conversions.
        
    *   Specify the number of [event types](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/event-types/event-types-explained)
         you want to track within each mobile app and their names.
        
    *   Indicate the type of commission you’ll offer partners for driving mobile events like app installs or purchases.
        
    
2.  **Technical Details**
    
    *   Indicate whether your mobile apps use a [Mobile Measurement Partner](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/mobile-app-tracking)
         ([MMP](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/mobile-app-tracking/mobile-tracking-using-mmps-explained)
        ) or [Customer Data Platform](https://www.twilio.com/en-us/resource-center/what-is-a-customer-data-platform)
         (CDP) to send conversion data.
        
    *   If you know it, specify the integration method for your mobile app.
        
    

Confirmation[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program#confirmation)

Review the details you’ve provided in all sections, then submit the checklist to have our team begin setup.

Request Received[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program#request-received)

Your request is complete. An impact.com representative will review your submission and be in touch.

Additional resources[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program#additional-resources)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Explore related resources to help you manage your programs after you set them up:

*   [Self-Serve Requests Overview](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/self-serve-requests-overview)
     — Explains how to use step-by-step checklists to request new features or add-ons for your account without contacting support.
    
*   [Manage Your Account Programs](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/program-management/manage-your-account-programs)
     — Covers how to submit, pause, deactivate, or close a program once it's been created.
    
*   [Invite & Manage Users](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users)
     — Add, configure, and manage user access, roles, and permissions for your impact.com account.
    
*   [Manage General Finance Settings as a Brand](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/finance/finance-settings/manage-general-finance-settings-as-a-brand)
     — Configure your account's global financial settings, including assigning your primary finance contact and other account-wide finance preferences.
    

[PreviousSelf-Serve Requests Overview](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/self-serve-requests-overview)
[NextRequest a New Event Type](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/create-a-new-event-type)

*   [Additional resources](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/self-serve-requests/request-a-new-program#additional-resources)

# Create a Partner Integration | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/beta-create-a-partner-integration.md)
.

**Note:** This functionality is in beta. Contact your CSM or our [support team](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml?createTicket=true&)
 if you'd like to test this feature.

The Partner Integrations Hub allows you to connect with technical partners who require a JavaScript integration with your website. These technical partner integrations make use of the [impact.com universal tracking tag (UTT)](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/javascript-tag-tracking/tracking-with-universal-tracking-tag-utt)
, a JavaScript tag that is placed on your website, to execute new code from the technical partner.

**Note:** You must [have UTT enabled](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/javascript-tag-tracking/tracking-with-universal-tracking-tag-utt)
 as a pre-requisite for making use of the Partner Integrations Hub, and have approval from your website's IT or Site Reliability teams before deploying a JavaScript tag.

#### Step 1: Add the integration details[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/beta-create-a-partner-integration#step-1-add-the-integration-details)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the _Account_ column, scroll to the _Technical_ heading and select **Partner Integrations Hub**.
    
3.  From the top-right of the page, select **Add Integration**.
    
4.  Select the integration's _Integration Tag_ from the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**.
    
    *   The identifiers required to set up an integration will differ from partner to partner. When you select a partner from the drop-down menu, you'll see which identifier the partner requires.
        
    *   If you have any questions, please reach out directly to the partner with which you are integrating.
        
    
5.  Enter any unique identifier required by the selected partner. For instance, in the screenshot below, the integration requires the _domain_ identifier.
    
6.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FwXho9QKP3e4iU4ZKUSJ5%252F167ebb8cc30303.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da046e9f0-63aa-46f6-8a35-f73973fd326c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da58aac3e10a80e67d81ac893e14f6f6&sv=3) **\[Checked box\]** **Email partner when integration is added** to send an email notification to the relevant technical partner.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FYj7ky1utgSvHvymuHEx9%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252015.57.22.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D08100879-ce23-4f94-81be-9c03a139c0a2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ac4f668d57166cb2a2253111d1af8e3b&sv=3)

1.  Next, select **Add Integration** to save the integration details and proceed to the review stage.
    

#### Step 2: Review and publish the integration[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/beta-create-a-partner-integration#step-2-review-and-publish-the-integration)

After adding the integration, the _Review and Publish_ page is displayed. This page includes a validation checklist to ensure you've addressed common technical and compliance requirements before the integration goes live. Completing this checklist helps verify several common implementation gaps and ensures your integration is published accurately and can be tracked successfully.

1.  To enable the _Publish Tag_ button, you must review and select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]** for each item listed below:
    
    Field
    
    Description
    
    Code Placement confirmed
    
    Verify that the impact.com Universal Tag is correctly placed on your website.
    
    If applicable, confirm that the partner's tag domain is allowed in your website's Content Security Policy (CSP) so the script isn't blocked.
    
    Correct Integration ID
    
    Validate the unique IDs (e.g., "id") entered are accurate and match those provided by your integration partner.
    
    Privacy & Compliance Review
    
    Confirm adherence to data privacy regulations (e.g. GDPR, CCPA) and other legal requirement, ensuring no personal data is inadvertently collected or shared.
    
    Internal Stakeholder Sign-Off
    
    Since new code will be placed on your website, secure approval from required internal stakeholders (e.g. Site reliability Engineer, Product manager, Legal) before publishing.
    
2.  Once the required confirmations are complete:
    
    *   Select **Publish Tag** to enable the integration immediately.
        
    *   Alternatively, if you are not ready to publish yet, select **Publish Later** to save the integration in a disabled state.
        
    

**Note:** Depending on your organization’s content security policy, you may need to add `https://*.partner.com` into your Content Security Policy directive. Replace`partner` with the top-level domain of the partner with which you are integrating.

#### Publish an added integration[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/beta-create-a-partner-integration#publish-an-added-integration)

If you've saved an integration in a disabled state, you can publish it by following these steps:

1.  From the [_Partner Integrations Hub_](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/fr/partner-integrations-hub.ihtml)
     page, find the _Disabled_ integration you want to publish.
    
2.  Hover your cursor over the integration and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **Publish**.
    
3.  After selecting _Publish_, the _Review and Publish_ page is displayed. Complete the verification steps outlined in Step 2: Review and publish the integration.
    
4.  The published integration is now enabled and will be displayed with an _Enabled_ status on the list in the _Partner Integrations Hub_.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FPPnWIMBIbSIx7wOJ9I0w%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252015.50.52.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dec0c9235-c63c-47c2-bb6a-32c89c8472f9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a5f62ea8371406dc68019f96650c9ca1&sv=3)

#### Update an integration[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/beta-create-a-partner-integration#update-an-integration)

You can edit an integration as follows:

1.  From the [_Partner Integrations Hub_](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/fr/partner-integrations-hub.ihtml)
     page, find the integration you want to edit.
    
2.  Hover your cursor over the integration and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **Edit**.
    
3.  Enter a new identifier, usually supplied by the technical partner, into the textbox.
    
4.  Select **Save** to save your changes.
    

[PreviousCreate Custom Partner Sign Up Terms for Brands](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/create-custom-partner-sign-up-terms-for-brands)
[NextProgram Settings](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings)

# Manage Reports | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/manage-reports.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1099652?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=fund-lp-150&utm_campaign=help-center)

When you open a report and change any view settings, you can save that report view or schedule it to be delivered to you. On the _More Reports_ screen, you can view all your reports, including saved views and scheduled reports, and you can pin specific reports to your left menu bar.

**Note:** Any modifications you make to reports will only be reflected for your user and not all account users.

#### Access More Reports[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/manage-reports#access-more-reports)

Each report on the _More Reports_ screen has a name with a corresponding _Description_ of what the report displays.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\] → Reports →** [More Reports](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/engage/fr/all_reports.ihtml)
    .
    
2.  On the _More Reports_ screen, hover over a report to do the following:
    
    *   To add or remove the report from your left navigation menu, select **Pin** or **Unpin**.
        
    *   To modify view settings, select **View** to open the report, adjust the filters according to your preferences, and select **Save** next to the report heading.
        
    *   Learn how to [save](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/save-reports)
        , [schedule](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/schedule-reports)
        , [download](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/download-a-report)
        , and [export](https://integrations.impact.com/brand-api-reference/reference/report-export/report-export)
         reports via API to always have the latest data for your account and programs.
        
    
3.  Select **Build Report** in the upper-right corner to create a custom report using [Data Lab](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/data-lab-introduction)
    .
    
4.  Select **Category** to filter reports by a specific category, e.g., _Performance_, _Finance_, or _Custom_ reports.
    
    *   Alternatively, enter a report name in the _search bar_ field to find a specific report.
        
    

[PreviousSave Reports](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/save-reports)
[NextPin a Report](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/report-management/pin-a-report)

# Create a Data Upload Connection | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection.md)
.

_Connectors_ are a system for all batch data file transfers between impact.com and brands, partners, or other parties. Each connection (an individual connector) has certain attributes, such as the data type (clicks, conversions, product catalogs, etc.), direction (inbound or outbound), method (email, SFTP, FTP), and more.

The Connector wizard allows you to set up connections to other platforms so that impact.com can automatically batch process the data for you. Either receiving data to enhance reporting in the impact.com platform or to deliver enriched data to your systems.

**Example**: Suppose you are collecting conversion data (i.e., Actions) on the server-side or through a cookie. In that case, you can submit your data to impact.com so you can visualize your program's performance, attribute credit to partners, and modify or reverse any actions before they lock.

#### Create an inbound connection[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection#create-an-inbound-connection)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  In the left column, under _Technical_, select **Connections**.
    
3.  Select **Create Connection** in the top right to access the Connector wizard.
    
    *   You can't delete a connection once it's been created.
        
    

Connector terminology[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection#connector-terminology)

Term

Description

Name

Provide a name that describes the connection. E.g., _Upload Aggregate Performance Data_.

Direction

The direction of the data that this connection supports: _Inbound_ is data coming into impact.com, _Outbound_ is data going out of impact.com to another platform.

Data Type

The type of data this connection processes (E.g., conversion data, action modifications, etc.).

Method

The transfer method this connection uses, E.g., FTP, SFTP, or Email.

Methodology

The transfer methodology. _Inbound_ connections support “Push to Impact” and “Pull from 3rd Party”. _Outbound_ connections support “Pull from Impact”.

Connection Details

The connection details that will be required is dependant on a combination of the chosen _Direction_, _Method_ and _Methodology_. In some cases the you will need to provide details and in other cases impact.com will provide the details.

#### Step 1: Direction[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection#step-1-direction)

1.  Enter the connection name and select the _Direction_ of the data, in this instance select **Give data to impact.com**.
    
    *   Read about **receiving data from impact.com**.
        
    
2.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fwo9AGWjuSu46OThOUDVm%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-16%2520at%252017.55.32.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8b71daf6-615c-4995-92d4-a4e569b074a4&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b11d21d921cdd282ab0251392e8de822&sv=3)

#### Step 2: Type[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection#step-2-type)

1.  Select the type of data that the connection will support, e.g., **Conversions**.
    
2.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FX6ez3jME0YSxD4SRc4aD%252F5367F56D-5ECD-487C-BE9A-DC2A2EF52B94.jpeg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2fbb4fce-9b25-4d64-9772-26476aefdca2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2e241d02d31f2cfafb7d72f9b7d0eb13&sv=3)

#### Step 3: Method[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection#step-3-method)

1.  Select the _Method_ to use to give data to impact.com: **Email**, **SFTP**, or **FTP**.
    
    *   The _Email_ method requires that you add a wildcard pattern that matches filenames of attachments within the email, e.g., `Conversions_202?_*.csv`.
        
        *   `*****` represents any sequence of characters.
            
        *   `**?**` represents any single character.
            
        
    
    **Warning:** All attachments in an email should match the wildcard pattern, or the email will be ignored. Attached files prefixed with `receipt_` or `result_` will be ignored, because they are reserved for receipt or result files.
    
2.  Select the _Methodology_ to use, either **Push to impact.com** or **Pull from third party**.
    
    *   The _Pull from third party_ methodology requires that you enter a **Folder** with the absolute directory path that contains the file, e.g., `/home/AcmeAdvertiser/Conversions`.
        
    
3.  **\[Toggle on\]** Toggle on **Encryption** to enable encryption for the connection. Encryption is only available for inbound connections (uploads to impact.com), not outbound (downloads from impact.com).
    
    *   Select **Generate New Key** to generate a new 2048-bit RSA [public/private key pair](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public-key_cryptography)
         . The key pair is valid for two years and is used for PGP encryption and decryption of your data files. You will receive the public key only, which must be used to encrypt your data files before uploading them to this connection.
        
    *   (Optional) You can select **Copy to clipboard** to store the new key on your clipboard.
        
    
4.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FBXrlGYZlGwJtlrgtlczb%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-17%2520at%252009.56.38.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddd43137a-fa7a-457f-9cef-6afb13a9de1b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=91c6d4507091c682cbff3eb1f2b248e8&sv=3)

#### Step 4: Credentials[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection#step-4-credentials)

1.  Either select existing credentials, or create new credentials.
    
    *   The screens to create credentials may look different depending on the selected method.
        
    *   You will see a popup displaying your provisioned credentials. Select **Copy** to proceed.
        
    

Email

SFTP

FTP

Enter the credential's name and the return email address, then click **Create**.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FAwTcji4ZBPzbrdjAXBR7%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-17%2520at%252010.03.19.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D977dd155-ce7d-4a29-865f-01e03c66af5e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=35d9daa1e3a43a27dcbf03fb0444b936&sv=3)

Enter the credential's name and click **Create**.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FEJFLf9TkVKnt4FgbwFl5%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-20%2520at%252010.15.51.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddabd6a45-1012-4247-968b-7288e702e2b1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=cfd8308d8123ada64cc640d47a366dea&sv=3)

Enter the credential's name and click **Create**.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FoiQ2OsDWQ6pvRaUdy4Rn%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-17%2520at%252017.57.57.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0566d1f2-7bcf-4e4c-a546-b5f88e3f0eed&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0f9694696eb9e857a4166b65844d8781&sv=3)

1.  Select **Next** to continue.
    

#### Step 5: Review[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/create-a-data-upload-connection#step-5-review)

Review the configuration to ensure all details are captured correctly and then select **Create** to create the connection. Once your connection has been created, you can use this connection to upload data to impact.com.

[PreviousData Connections Overview](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/data-connections-overview)
[NextConfigure a Data Download Connection](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/send-data-to-impactcom/configure-a-data-download-connection)

# Create & Share Tracking Links with Partners | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/create-and-share-tracking-links-with-partners.md)
.

You can assist your partners with creating tracking or vanity links and sharing them on an ad-hoc basis.

#### Create a tracking link[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/create-and-share-tracking-links-with-partners#create-a-tracking-link)

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Partners** → **Partners**.
    
2.  From your partner list, hover over the partner for whom you want to create a tracking link, then select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\]** → **Tracking** → **Create a link**.
    
3.  Optionally, specify an alternative landing page and select **Advanced** to add Sub IDs, a [Shared ID](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/contracts-and-template-terms/contracts/dynamically-pay-partners-with-the-shared-id)
    , or a verified partner property to the tracking link for reporting purposes.
    
    **Note:** Sub IDs are optional and partners use them for additional tracking values.
    
4.  Select **Create**.
    
    *   To edit the URL protocol, select the **URL protocol** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**.
        
        *   **https://**—The link will only use the secure version of HTTP.
            
        *   **http(s)://**—The link will attempt to use the secure version of HTTP and will use unsecured HTTP as a fallback.
            
        
    *   To create a custom short link (aka a vanity link), select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-f80b4972f1093fe676c65fe5018fb55ca5c00308%252F059dd4194b579d17193227e33dfa1fe0475b0089a93e202b16f8902ffc532178.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d8bc7bc26dd5168727c767e4c2098660&sv=3) **\[Edit\]**, change the URL path, and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-4e2eb56f7a0317e4afab899bcc5728b41c936979%252F156d94b4eae47d787101ad70424e4db735832f2f96a09cbe3ccfa3e08e408b05.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2d3c3e6c4620be83d2c05ba05fb015a3&sv=3) **\[Checkmarked\]**.
        
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FjXBvfyKDGC86SLrdL3tL%252Facme.sjv.ioAb1CCd.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De828dacc-dad6-4b6d-a96b-11a82d098ba7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=676873646f3176c5078e0878a90ac4ca&sv=3)
    

#### Share a tracking link[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/create-and-share-tracking-links-with-partners#share-a-tracking-link)

You can copy the link to your clipboard or share the QR code with partners.

**Copy link to clipboard**

1.  Select the link.
    
    *   You will receive a message saying that the link has been copied to clipboard.
        
    
2.  Share the copied link with your partners.
    

**Share the QR code**

1.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-2b31470878090ec5200e5b89d7c37af32da95420%252F610de3aca20bfbff9c56352b3711f2689242e264b7d29de8d01abae74337ea09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=68d260a96b4cce79435057a04f2cc78d&sv=3) **\[QR code\]**.
    
2.  You can take a screenshot of the QR code and manually share it with your partners.
    

[PreviousCreate a Custom Widget](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/create-a-custom-widget)
[NextReceive Leads from Your Partners](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/receive-leads-from-your-partners)

# Create Custom Measures in Data Lab | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/create-custom-measures-in-data-lab.md)
.

**Important:** We _highly_ recommend you reference [Data Lab Introduction](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/data-lab-introduction)
 before starting this article.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\] → Reports** → **Data Lab**.
    
2.  Select a **visualization** to get started.
    
3.  From the Dimensions and Measures section in the right toolbar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-6282f271b7521383385339243690b303086736d9%252F3b5a585dd2895bdf1c30d1236c72ba590dddd18717863b27074c8d313d05ae20.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=58f674ab9ed407918c2350dfa2328fba&sv=3) **\[Create Custom Measure\]**.
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FaJ9k8ckxQGr9Bokgj8Mb%252FUntitled%2520design%2520%2812%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D68dfc0f3-adb5-4c88-8447-6ceafc61b73d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5ebc027490c180aa46ad22d63d7dd6c8&sv=3)
    
4.  Name your custom Measure — the example below will show a measure called _Conversion Rate_.
    
5.  Select your measure’s **Format**. You can choose between _Number_, _String_, _Percentage_, or _Currency_.
    
6.  In the _Formula_ field, enter the calculation for the custom measure.
    
    **Example:** The _Conversion Rate_ measure will use `Actions Costs / Clicks`. impact.com will specify whether the formula is valid or not by a small ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-4e2eb56f7a0317e4afab899bcc5728b41c936979%252F156d94b4eae47d787101ad70424e4db735832f2f96a09cbe3ccfa3e08e408b05.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2d3c3e6c4620be83d2c05ba05fb015a3&sv=3) **\[check mark\]** at the top-right of the window.
    
7.  Select **Save** to save your custom measure.
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FeRSxwBeihOE4jt2Um20f%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-27%2520at%252017.31.58.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D805ccf5a-b8b8-499d-86e7-efc04994dc80&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e68fef41e5e8df1b4642230e4c437d90&sv=3)
    

[PreviousData Lab Visualizations Explained](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/data-lab-visualizations-explained)
[NextEdit a Custom Report](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/edit-a-custom-report)

# Share a Custom Report | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/share-a-custom-report.md)
.

You can assign different roles and permissions and share your custom report with other users in your account.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Reports** → **More Reports**.
    
2.  Select **Category** and filter for **Built** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]**.
    
3.  Find and select a custom report that you’ve created.
    
4.  In the top-right corner, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-34bd132ae3d7a7f510a92ff492a692f93980595c%252F642c4d94b1fedffb1439be1abf197d6e67db17d153c6f87493a5b3e77f251db1.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e2cbc9840da2e0311728cf253d8be3ee&sv=3) **\[Sharing\]**.
    
    *   This icon will only be visible on custom reports that you own or where you’ve been given the owner's permission.
        
    
5.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-6282f271b7521383385339243690b303086736d9%252F3b5a585dd2895bdf1c30d1236c72ba590dddd18717863b27074c8d313d05ae20.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=58f674ab9ed407918c2350dfa2328fba&sv=3) **\[Add\]** to give members access to the report and start typing the names of these members.
    
    *   The names will auto-fill once you start typing.
        
    
6.  Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-df93ac0f80fc5cece7983980a5ce3fbcf19aabce%252F3ef9d737089bc07dbb7e4fd41a97edfbaec261e4f55ab3a1666f3daf957f69c3.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7156f55b40b1371c0823c4c12cd99f7a&sv=3) **\[Drop-down\]** and choose a specific permission.
    
    *   **Owner:** Owners can edit or delete a report or change other users' permissions.
        
    *   **Editor:** Editors can edit the existing report.
        
    *   **Viewer:** Viewers can only view the existing report.
        
    *   **Remove:** Remove a member’s report access.
        
    
7.  Select **Add members**.
    
8.  Select **Save**.
    
    *   Optionally, select **Copy Report Link** to copy a direct link to the report on your clipboard.
        
    

**General access:** Check **All members in the account can view this report** to give everyone in this account viewing permissions.

[PreviousEdit a Custom Report](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/edit-a-custom-report)
[NextEmail a Custom Report](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/multi-program-reports/data-lab-custom-reports/email-a-custom-report)

# Connect Your Personal Email to Your impact.com Account | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/emails/connect-your-personal-email-to-your-impactcom-account.md)
.

**Important:** This feature is only accessible to Professional and Enterprise editions.

Connect your personal email to your impact.com account to [send emails to prospective partners](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/performance-program/recruiting-partners/prospective-partners/contact-a-prospect)
 directly from your email address. This adds a more personal feel to your recruiting and outreach efforts.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select your ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User Profile\]** → **Edit Profile**.
    
2.  Under the _Send as email address_ section, select **Connect your email**.
    
3.  Select your email provider and enter your email address.
    
4.  Select **Next**.
    
5.  Authenticate the account by completing any multifactor authentication step.
    
6.  impact.com will request that you **Sign in to Mediarails**. Mediarails is an impact.com company and was acquired by impact.com in September 2018. Select **Continue**.
    
7.  Select **Select all** to give impact.com the necessary permissions to send emails on your behalf.
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-91d9884e694916b30c4aedc913663b92804604eb%252F964ad81222aff04a657799371046be2eb29c8b1e0b78b13619b103f1b5ebe573.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c9af36175daa8fb89caf65ad90ca6d7b&sv=3)
    
8.  Select **Continue**.
    
    *   Your email will now be connected to your impact.com account. This is denoted by the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-3cf3c05ed3805d25beec58d032bb013b22d5d33d%252F15c44d5f4ea08ada8bd67240c1006604f768638ba1169cc3d1d435fd8c73a9c5.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=29fa5a4b44e4db271993472604633c63&sv=3) **Email connected** confirmation below your email address.
        
    
    ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FtHkVlxAyyNXfku37xRk6%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-24%2520at%252010.31.35.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dabd0eaeb-65ad-40d4-8d90-2b83dc7d6bf1&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0d5ff5505be055d52613b39e89463739&sv=3)
    
9.  Select **Save** to finalise the changes made to your profile.
    

#### Supported email providers[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/emails/connect-your-personal-email-to-your-impactcom-account#supported-email-providers)

impact.com supports integrating with the following email providers:

*   Gmail
    
*   Microsoft Office 365
    

[PreviousEmails](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/emails)
[NextEmail a Partner](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/emails/email-a-partner)

# Create & Manage Partner Groups | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/create-and-manage-partner-groups.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1117599?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=eng-350&utm_campaign=help-center)

Use _Partner Groups_ to help organize your partners into labeled groups. When partners are in a group, you can offer them unique ad content and template terms (contracts) that aren't available to your other partners. You can also send targeted communication to partner groups.

**Note:** Partner Groups are only visible in your impact.com brand account — partners can't see groups (names, other members, etc.) at all.

Create a group[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/create-and-manage-partner-groups#create-a-group)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You'll need to first create a partner group before you can add partners.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Partners →** [**Groups**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/engage/mediapartners/view-mediapartnergroups-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
2.  In the upper-right corner, select **Create Group**.
    
3.  Enter **a name** for the partner group in the text box.
    
4.  Select **Submit**.
    

Manage partner groups[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/create-and-manage-partner-groups#manage-partner-groups)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Manage your existing partner groups by performing the following actions below:

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Partners →** [**Groups**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/engage/mediapartners/view-mediapartnergroups-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
2.  Hover over each partner group to perform various actions and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FaMIlbqH54V4Ow5JvT0I0%252F%255BMore%255D%2520vNext%25202.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D6d5449e8-e413-48fa-b059-58ebb3e2c271&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=08be405cf599953e308d795c12cfb1cc&sv=3) **\[More\]**.
    
    Action
    
    Description
    
    Edit
    
    Make adjustments to the name of an existing partner group and select **Save** to confirm your change.
    
    Delete
    
    Read the pop-up notification and select **Delete** to confirm the action.
    
    Download Email List
    
    Download the email list of partners in the partner group.
    

[PreviousPartner Groups](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups)
[NextBulk Edit Partners in a Group](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/bulk-edit-partners-in-a-group)
