# Gateway Tracking Templates | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates.md)
.

**Important:** Refer to [Configure Gateway Tracking Settings](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings)
 before continuing with this help article.

#### Program Tracking Template[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates#program-tracking-template)

*   This is where you can define query string parameters for all tracking links within your program. These parameters will be applied to links from all of your media sources and partners. Refer to the _Query string parameters reference_ below for help.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FbDxnCa31osyQYVDuWuwr%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252016.38.46.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D10cb955b-9f1d-458b-8202-e54a150930df&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=962d7f73286b9c4f1ce08d02a0e5584e&sv=3)

#### Partner Tracking Template[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates#partner-tracking-template)

*   This is where you can add global query string parameters to partner tracking links. If a click comes from a partner's tracking link, the parameters you establish in the template will be applied. This is a good place to add UTM Parameters. Learn how to [Collect Campaign Data with Google Analytics](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/set-up-tracking/collect-campaign-data-with-google-analytics)
     using UTM parameters. Refer to the _Query string parameters reference_ below for help.
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FifK7GKmmxfT7BlPUhu4H%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252017.05.22.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da1dc3868-e027-4699-8872-9d424de79272&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ecf252fb3f2ddff3641afe1919a9c304&sv=3)

#### Query string parameters for tracking templates[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates#query-string-parameters-for-tracking-templates)

*   Parameters with curly brackets ({}) are dynamic query strings that will automatically populate with a value when the link is clicked
    
*   Hardcoded values are supported—simply add a value without curly brackets ({}), and it will always be passed on a click.
    
*   Custom values are supported, however, we recommend [contacting support](https://app.impact.com/support/portal.ihtml?createTicket=true)
     to discuss how to use them.
    
*   Use underscores to add multiple parameters within a single query string.
    

Query string parameters reference[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates#query-string-parameters-reference)

URL Parameter

Example

Description

`{clickid}`

2uz3sQWFlxyJRcqwUx 0Mo34VUkiyviUnPwZbQM0

**Click ID**

Unique ID assigned by impact.com associated with the click event. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the unique click ID associated with the customer.

`{iradid}`

828416

**impact.com Ad ID**

Unique ID associated with the ad that is associated with the click event. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the Ad ID associated with the click.

`{ircid}`

10306

**impact.com Campaign/Program ID**

Unique ID associated with your impact.com program (campaign). When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the campaign/program ID associated with the click.

`{irpid}`

1234567

**impact.com Partner ID**

Unique ID assigned to the partner that drove the click. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the partner ID associated with the click.

`{iradname}`

My Ad

**impact.com Ad Name**

The name you've assigned to the ad that was associated with the click. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the ad's name.

`{iradtype}`

Banner

**impact.com Ad Type**

When you create ads in impact.com, you define the type of the ad (e.g., banner, text, image) before uploading creative. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the ad type.

`{iradsize}`

100x100

**impact.com Ad Size**

impact.com requires you to specify an ad's size. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the size of the ad that you specified when it was created.

`{prodsku}`

123ABC987

**Product SKU**

Unique SKU assigned to the product associated with the ad. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the product SKU value associated with the ad.

`{irmpname}`

CoyoteInfluencer

**impact.com Partner Name**

The name of the partner in impact.com. When a tracking link is clicked, this will populate with the partner's name associated with the click.

**Note:** Partners may have duplicate names or names with special characters, which will likely skew your data analytics findings. To ensure accurate data, use `{irpid}` instead. (You can look up partner IDs on the [_Partner Listing Report_](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/report/viewReport.report?handle=adv_mp_date_joined)
.)

`{irmptype}`

mediapartner

**impact.com Media Type**

This will return either _mediapartner_ or _mediasource_ depending where the click originated from—either from one of your partners, or from one of your media sources (such as traffic from a search engine).

`{sharedid}`

**Shared ID**

Partners can append a Shared ID value as a query string parameter to their own tracking links, which is then visible to you in reporting. When a tracking link is clicked, this will automatically populate with the Shared ID a partner has specified, if available.

`{mp_value1}`, `{mp_value2}`, `{mp_value3}`

Deal

**Partner Custom Value**

This will populate with a custom value you can assign to a partner. **This setting is applied across your entire account, not just for a single Program/Campaign**.

Custom values can be assigned to partners in **Partners → Partners → Actions → Tracking**. From here, you can define up to three custom values for each partner. These are most commonly used to label partners (e.g., influencer, Cashback/Loyalty, Deal, etc.).

You can also bulk upload and edit partner custom values in **Partners → Partners → Bulk Edit**. You will need an Excel or CSV file with the Partner IDs as well as the Partner Values themselves that you want to assign to that partner.

`{timestamp}`

1598556546

**Timestamp**

Generates the UNIX timestamp when the click event occurred. When a tracking link is clicked, this will be appended as a query string parameter. This can be used as a cache-buster in some platforms.

`{randint}`

1,234,567

**Random Integer**

Generates a random integer between 0–9,999,999 when a tracking link is clicked.

`{impacttest}`

1

**Test**

Append this parameter when you test your tracking links.

`{irmpgroupname}`

”influencers”,”preferred”

**Group name**

Lists one or multiple groups that a partner belongs to within a query string.

`{CUSTOM}`

**Custom**

Use a custom dynamic query string parameter. impact.com recommends contacting support (or your CSM) to discuss your specific use case.

#### Parameter deduplication explained[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates#parameter-deduplication-explained)

The various parameters specified for an ad in a program can sometimes lead to duplicate values being returned, especially when multiple parameters have the same name. Duplicate parameter values can create a discrepancy between impact.com's reporting and the reporting of your wider marketing team's tools, such as Google Analytics. To avoid this, impact.com considers only a single parameter value in the following order of priority (from highest to lowest).

This order prioritizes brand-specified parameters over partner-specified ones:

Parameter type reference[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates#parameter-type-reference)

Parameter type

Description

Ad landing page

The parameters you configure within your ad’s landing page metadata. (These parameters always take precedence unless a deep link is provided with the request, in which case deep link params override the landing page params.)

Program tracking template

The parameters you configure under **Settings → Gateway** (found in the _Tracking_ section) → **Program Tracking Template**.

Partner gateway

The parameters you configure under **Settings → Gateway** (found in the _Tracking_ section) → **Program Tracking Template**.

Passthrough variable

The parameters that your partners can specify within a deep link, using the `p.` notation. (In rare cases, passthrough parameters can override parameters that you specify.)

Deep link query string

The parameters that your partners can use to pass a deep link containing pre-defined landing page query string parameters.

Example[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/gateway-tracking-templates#example)

If the `utm_medium` parameter is appended to both the landing page deep link used by a partner and your own _Program Tracking Template_ as shown below:

*   _Deep Link URL:_ `https://brand.example?utm_medium=seo`
    
*   _Program Tracking Template:_ `https://brand.example?utm_medium=affiliate`
    
    The final landing page will be:
    
*   _Program Tracking Template:_ `https://brand.example?utm_medium=``**affiliate**`
    
    This is because the _Program Tracking Template_ parameter was defined by you and is thus given a higher priority than the one defined by your partner.
    

[PreviousConfigure Gateway Tracking Settings](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/configure-gateway-tracking-settings)
[NextSet Third Party Gateway URLs & Click Tracking](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/tracking-settings/set-third-party-gateway-urls-and-click-tracking)

Last updated 1 month ago

# Receive Leads from Your Partners | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/receive-leads-from-your-partners.md)
.

One of the best ways to find the right partners to partner with is by receiving leads from existing ones. By integrating your third-party CRM system lead forms into your partners’ Dashboards, you can leverage your existing partnerships in impact.com to generate leads.

#### Serve a third-party lead form to your partners[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/receive-leads-from-your-partners#serve-a-third-party-lead-form-to-your-partners)

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Content → Widgets**.
    
2.  In the top-right corner, select **Create Widget → Lead Form**.
    
3.  Enter the **Name** of the lead form.
    
4.  Optionally, enter a **Description** for the lead form.
    
5.  Select the **Source** of your third-party lead form.
    
    *   Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-590bdf4e1ffbcc1fe86ad456bebab6a28c64af1f%252Ffd6e4291628446bb1fe27a831f7874b256a28cc84833d72cce2e73b94641edb7.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c0ae24100ce670ad15e57d05fb25b1b1&sv=3) **\[Radio Button\]** **URL** and enter the lead form URL. Partners who use the lead form with a URL source will be automatically attributed with the lead.
        
    *   Alternatively, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-590bdf4e1ffbcc1fe86ad456bebab6a28c64af1f%252Ffd6e4291628446bb1fe27a831f7874b256a28cc84833d72cce2e73b94641edb7.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c0ae24100ce670ad15e57d05fb25b1b1&sv=3) **\[Radio Button\]** **Embed Code** and enter the embed code into the field provided.
        
        *   If you select **Embed Code** as your lead form source, you must add the partner ID token `irpid={irpid}` in your embed code to track and attribute leads to partners. If you select **URL** as your lead form source, the token is automatically appended as a query parameter, so leads will automatically be attributed to partners who submit them.
            
        
    *   See examples of a source _URL_ and _Embed Code_ below.
        
        **Example Source URL:**
        
        `https://share.hsforms.com/1234_0AbCDE1FgHIjKLmnop2q`
        
        **Example Source Embed Code:**
        
        AskCopy
        
            <script charset="utf-8" type="text/javascript" 
            src="//js.hsforms.net/forms/shell.js"></script> 
            <script> 
            irpid={irpid}; 
            </script> 
            <script> 
            hbspt.forms.create({ 
            region: "sa1", 
            portalId: "12345678", 
            formId: "e0e27fd0-8a84-446d-92b4-952450e76a6a" 
            }); 
            </script>
        
    
6.  Optionally, ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** **Restrict Partner Access** to set which partners can use this lead form. You can restrict the lead form to specific _Partners_ or _Partner Groups_.
    
7.  Optionally, add **Labels** to your lead form.
    
8.  Set the **Language** of the lead form.
    
9.  Select **Save**.
    
    *   Optionally, select **Preview** for a preliminary display of the lead form.
        
    

Once you’ve created the lead form widget, it will become available to partners who are partnered with you as a widget on their dashboard.

**Note:** Refer to the [HubSpot integration](https://integrations.impact.com/integration-guides/for-brands/plugin-integrations/crm-customer-relationship-management/integrate-with-hubspot)
 to track lead form submissions and monitor changes in status for contacts and deals (such as a lead turning into a sale) in impact.com.

[PreviousCreate & Share Tracking Links with Partners](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/generate-leads/create-and-share-tracking-links-with-partners)
[NextSegments](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/segments)

# Manage Your Marketplace Profile as a Brand | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/2120798?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=&utm_campaign=help-center)

Your Marketplace Profile is the first impression potential partners will have of your brand. It’s not only how you communicate what your brand is about, but also the details of your program and the type of partners you’re looking to collaborate with. The brand marketplace is the place to get potential partners excited about working with you, so you should take great care with what you include here.

Marketplace best practices[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands#marketplace-best-practices)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Be complete:** Fill out every field so there are no gaps in your image or program details.
    
*   **Convey who you are:** Craft a succinct brand snapshot (“why we exist”) to give emotional context.
    
*   **Define your ideal partners:** Specify audience demographics, content styles, and engagement thresholds.
    
*   **Use keyword‑rich descriptions:** Include terms your target partners might search.
    
*   **Keep your profile fresh:** Review and update it quarterly or whenever you launch a new product or campaign.
    

Manage your marketplace profile[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands#manage-your-marketplace-profile)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You should ensure your marketplace profile looks and reads exactly how you want it to. The following steps will show you how to edit your marketplace profile.

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252F210dEQa93qyQgoJDkieL%252FUser%2520profile.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df84c5b17-fae4-4a30-990a-9de5e57a150b&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d54584f2509b3c46e401547cd2324ed0&sv=3) **\[User profile\] → Settings**.
    
2.  On the right, under _General_, select [**Marketplace Profile**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/campaign/view-edit-campaign-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
3.  Complete each section of the profile. These sections will be visible on your profile card for partners to view:
    

**Get discovered by the right partners:** Partners search the marketplace for brands that match their specific vision, such as `electronic brands on Amazon` or `clean beauty brands`. Fill out your _Marketplace Profile_ completely to ensure you don't miss out on these searches. Learn more about Marketplace Search [here](https://help.impact.com/other/reference-documentation/marketplace-search-explained)
.

Program Information[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands#program-information)

Program Information

Description

Name

Enter the name of your program. This will be the name that appears wherever the program is referenced, like in reports.

Description

Enter a more detailed description of your program. Partners can read this before applying to your brand. Maximum 250 characters.

Logo

Select **Choose File** to upload an image (e.g., company logo) in .png, .jpeg, .jpg, or .gif format. This image appears in the Brands Marketplace and on the _Program Overview_ page. Recommended minimum size: 150 x 400 pixels.

Service Areas

Select your service areas from the ![[Drop-down menu]](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fpaligoapp-cdn-eu1.s3.eu-west-1.amazonaws.com%2Fimpact%2Fattachments%2Ff01cdffa431a4d75ff09c130b66974d4-d6b35853a9aa1f1fe6bd66da29bd09fa.svg&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2d59277317608df5f7fd13d877bf4959&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** to specify where you can provide goods and services to assist partners with consumer targeting.

Primary Category

Select the primary industry (or industries) from the ![[Drop-down menu]](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fpaligoapp-cdn-eu1.s3.eu-west-1.amazonaws.com%2Fimpact%2Fattachments%2Ff01cdffa431a4d75ff09c130b66974d4-d6b35853a9aa1f1fe6bd66da29bd09fa.svg&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2d59277317608df5f7fd13d877bf4959&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** that best describe your products or services. Providing this information can help partners find your program faster.

Secondary Category (Optional)

Select another industry (or industries) from the ![[Drop-down menu]](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fpaligoapp-cdn-eu1.s3.eu-west-1.amazonaws.com%2Fimpact%2Fattachments%2Ff01cdffa431a4d75ff09c130b66974d4-d6b35853a9aa1f1fe6bd66da29bd09fa.svg&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2d59277317608df5f7fd13d877bf4959&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** that best describes your products or services. Providing this information can help partners find your program faster.

Does your business offer any of the following? (Optional)

Some partners are restricted in what they can promote. Select the ![[Unchecked box]](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fpaligoapp-cdn-eu1.s3.eu-west-1.amazonaws.com%2Fimpact%2Fattachments%2Ff01cdffa431a4d75ff09c130b66974d4-396370c2cdec89ba7d714c4c58e6845b.svg&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=17a57bf02e8769a3e43d310efa5d5a8f&sv=3) **\[Unchecked boxes\]** below to find better matching partners:

*   CBD
    
*   Vaping
    
*   Adult Content
    
*   Gambling
    

Partner Business Model

Describes how a partner generates revenue. Select the type of partners with whom you want to work.

**Business categories explained** **Social Influencer**

As a creator, you have an audience that follows you for entertainment, education, and community, and they engage with social-native content that you produce. You may market products and services to this audience via social media platforms.

**Content/Reviews**

Partners focused on producing editorial content related to promoting, comparing and listing products and services.

**Loyalty/Rewards**

Partners who incentivize transactions from consumers, employees, or businesses through a membership and benefits reward program.

**Deal/Coupons**

Partners who aggregate and classify deals and promotions for consumer savings.

**Email/Newsletter**

Partners who have an audience that subscribes to their email or newsletter that brings together valuable content, discounts, and other information.

**Search/Comparison**

Search Engine, Social, or Programmatic marketers that manage keyword campaigns for brands, often on a performance basis.

**Network**

Partner platforms that broker access to brand campaigns and provide tracking, reporting, and payment services.

**Technology Solution**

Site-side shopping tools or services that drive conversion optimization for brands.

**Cross Audience Monetization**

Businesses that publish offers, content, and complementary products to current customers or audiences (e.g., exit traffic, improved UX) to drive incremental revenue.

A partner is a great match if.... (Optional)

Describe what you find makes a suitable partner. Maximum 100 characters.

Features you are offering (Optional)

Choose which feature/s you offer as a brand by selecting the ![[Unchecked box]](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fpaligoapp-cdn-eu1.s3.eu-west-1.amazonaws.com%2Fimpact%2Fattachments%2Ff01cdffa431a4d75ff09c130b66974d4-396370c2cdec89ba7d714c4c58e6845b.svg&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=17a57bf02e8769a3e43d310efa5d5a8f&sv=3) **\[Unchecked boxes\]**:

*   Placement Opportunities
    
*   Send Free Products
    
*   Average Order Value
    
*   Work with Creators
    
*   Gift Card Payouts
    
*   Trademark Bidding
    

Search Keywords

Create a Brands Marketplace SEO list. Separate keywords with commas. Do not include spaces. Maximum 145 characters.

Contacts[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands#contacts)

Contact

Description

Company Contacts

Select one of your account users to act as a [primary marketplace contact](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/who-is-my-primary-marketplace-contact)
.

Agency Contacts

If an agency will manage this program and they will handle partner management, select an agency contact.

Program Visibility[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands#program-visibility)

Program Visibility

Description

Show this profile

Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]** to show your profile in the brand marketplace.

Servicing Agency

If an agency manages this program, you can publish their information in the Brands Marketplace. Learn how to [invite agency users to your account](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/invite-account-users)
.

• Select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b5e1740618ed6571d97b8fed7c25a7678f85d375%252F299632fb6f4f91fbf9847471754cc6180e4284a65b4960af4c4ea472a159f552.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2c048f1f88e16174bcf48e6e3f2c3264&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]** if want to display your servicing agency in the marketplace.

Program Resources[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands#program-resources)

Program Resources

Description

Company Homepage

Enter the link to your company's website landing page.

Example Landing Page (Optional)

Enter the link to a page that a consumer would land on if they clicked on an ad you provided to partners.

Program Information (Optional)

Enter the link to a page that gives potential partners more information about your program.

Social Accounts (Optional)

Optionally ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-6282f271b7521383385339243690b303086736d9%252F3b5a585dd2895bdf1c30d1236c72ba590dddd18717863b27074c8d313d05ae20.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=58f674ab9ed407918c2350dfa2328fba&sv=3) **\[Add\]** to **Add Another** social account you want to share with partners.

Enter the link to any social profile you want potential partners to be able to view about your company or program.

• Facebook

• Twitter/X

• Instagram

• Pinterest

• LinkedIn

• Youtube

• Tiktok

• Twitch

• Facebook

• Twitter/X

• Instagram

Additional Information[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/program-settings/profile-and-branding/manage-your-marketplace-profile-for-brands#additional-information)

*   **Links (Optional):** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** to add additional links you want to share with potential partners access.
    
    *   Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-6282f271b7521383385339243690b303086736d9%252F3b5a585dd2895bdf1c30d1236c72ba590dddd18717863b27074c8d313d05ae20.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=58f674ab9ed407918c2350dfa2328fba&sv=3) **\[Add\]** to **Add Another**.
        
    
*   **PDF Files (Optional):** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FiZjM1xY8o3v1iR3Zaysd%252FToggle.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db1550d48-bba9-4e81-8604-bc12370c5caa&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3dd155c02bb7170ae7b9567b915d37a3&sv=3) **\[Toggle on\]** to add additional PDF files that can give potential partners more information. Maximum file size is 7MB.
    
    *   Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-6282f271b7521383385339243690b303086736d9%252F3b5a585dd2895bdf1c30d1236c72ba590dddd18717863b27074c8d313d05ae20.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=58f674ab9ed407918c2350dfa2328fba&sv=3) **\[Add\]** to **Add Another**.
        
    

1.  Select **Save**.

# Manage Tasks | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks.md)
.

In your impact.com Performance account, you can use the _Tasks_ system to create assignable tasks for both your partners and your colleagues in your impact.com account. Learn how to view the tasks modal, create new tasks, update existing tasks, and leave comments on tasks.

**Don't see this feature?** Reach out to your account manager about updating your account permissions to view or manage tasks.

#### Create a task[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks#create-a-task)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-461dad8362fa40481525db4b8b814cf39c09fe76%252F6cd627787097989824963d4a8e0f3b3fe2b5aa99d0785c0cb5e327d70289a59a.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3468e81a4def941b083379737743373c&sv=3) **\[Tasks\]**.
    
2.  At the bottom of the tasks modal, select **Add task**.
    
3.  Fill in the necessary fields using the _Field reference_ below and select **Save** to save your task.
    

Field reference[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks#field-reference)

Field

Description

**Task Name**

Enter a name for the task.

**Description**

Optionally, add a description for this task that includes all relevant details.

**Task Type**

Use the drop-down menu to select the task type. If ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FwXho9QKP3e4iU4ZKUSJ5%252F167ebb8cc30303.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da046e9f0-63aa-46f6-8a35-f73973fd326c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da58aac3e10a80e67d81ac893e14f6f6&sv=3) **\[Checked box\]** _Task requires approval_ is selected, you'll need to manually approve it when the assignee marks it as complete. If this option isn't selected, a task will automatically be approved once the assignee marks the task as complete.

**Assign To**

Tasks can be assigned to partners or your team members on your brand account. When you select a partner account, you can use the second drop-down to select a specific member for that partner account or leave it blank to assign the task to anyone with access to the partner account. Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FwXho9QKP3e4iU4ZKUSJ5%252F167ebb8cc30303.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da046e9f0-63aa-46f6-8a35-f73973fd326c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da58aac3e10a80e67d81ac893e14f6f6&sv=3) **\[Checked box\]** _Hide task from partner_ if you want to hide the task from a partner.

**Choose File**

Select **Choose File** to attach files with no size limits. Supported formats:

• JPG

• PNG

• DOC

• MP4

• MOV

• PPT

• SAZ

• XLSX

• ZIP

**Due on**

Optionally, use the calendar modal to choose a due date and time of day. Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FwXho9QKP3e4iU4ZKUSJ5%252F167ebb8cc30303.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da046e9f0-63aa-46f6-8a35-f73973fd326c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da58aac3e10a80e67d81ac893e14f6f6&sv=3) **\[Checked box\]** _Mark as urgent_ to signify to the assignee that this task is considered urgent.

#### View tasks[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks#view-tasks)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-461dad8362fa40481525db4b8b814cf39c09fe76%252F6cd627787097989824963d4a8e0f3b3fe2b5aa99d0785c0cb5e327d70289a59a.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3468e81a4def941b083379737743373c&sv=3) **\[Tasks\]**.
    
2.  To view all tasks assigned to you or reported by you, switch between the tabs:
    
    *   From the _Assigned to you_ tab, select **View all tasks**.
        
    *   From the _Reported by you_ tab, select **View all tasks**.
        
    
3.  On the _Tasks_ screen, you can:
    
    *   **Filter** for specific tasks using the top filter bar.
        
    *   Select an individual task **name** to view a summary of the task.
        
    *   Select **Add Task** to create a new task.
        
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FZVjzVbfZACjAjkMvWPrT%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-08%2520at%252014.54.54.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D44c3a8c0-349a-4e5b-bab9-70f9204178d9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2e8a62d39f2bfe356a933448381ee4d1&sv=3)

#### Action a task item[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks#action-a-task-item)

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-461dad8362fa40481525db4b8b814cf39c09fe76%252F6cd627787097989824963d4a8e0f3b3fe2b5aa99d0785c0cb5e327d70289a59a.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3468e81a4def941b083379737743373c&sv=3) **\[Tasks\]**.
    
2.  In the tasks modal, select **Assigned to you**.
    
3.  Select one of your assigned tasks, and in the upper-right corner, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-df93ac0f80fc5cece7983980a5ce3fbcf19aabce%252F3ef9d737089bc07dbb7e4fd41a97edfbaec261e4f55ab3a1666f3daf957f69c3.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7156f55b40b1371c0823c4c12cd99f7a&sv=3) **\[Drop-down arrow\] → Start**.
    
    *   Alternatively, on the _Tasks_ screen, hover over your assigned task and select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FMLtRUwWz0pujlfmYWOgg%252FMoreVN.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03a3c355-a347-4207-baf7-c44fadf6d080&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9c1bd38fef98381022245f1801ffb314&sv=3) **\[More\] → Start**.
        
    *   See the _Actions reference below_ for more details on each action.
        
    

Actions reference[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks#actions-reference)

Action

Description

Start

Marks the task as _In Progress_.

Invalid

Marks the task as _Invalid_. Task reporters and the assignee can comment on the task to discuss why it's invalid.

Duplicate

Marks the task as duplicate. Assigners and assignees can comment on the task to discuss why it’s considered a duplicate.

Blocked

Marks the task as blocked. Assigners and assignees can comment on the task to discuss why they’re unable to complete the task.

Rejected

Marks the task as rejected. Assigners and assignees can comment on the task to discuss why it was rejected.

Completed

Marks the task as completed. The assignee must have already selected _Start_ on the task in order to mark it completed. Tasks that don't require approval will automatically have an _Approved_ status when marked as completed.

Delete

Deletes the task. Only assigners can delete tasks that they’ve created.

Archive

Archives the task. Only available on tasks that have been completed.

Reopen

Reopens a task that was moved from _In Progress_ to any other status mentioned above.

#### Update tasks[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks#update-tasks)

To add progress updates:

1.  From the top navigation bar, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-461dad8362fa40481525db4b8b814cf39c09fe76%252F6cd627787097989824963d4a8e0f3b3fe2b5aa99d0785c0cb5e327d70289a59a.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3468e81a4def941b083379737743373c&sv=3) **\[Tasks\]** → **View All**.
    
2.  To update the task progress, select a task to open it and add a **Comment** with an optional ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-8a8d53b8213cbc775f140da1793eae45e545aea1%252Fac7713ef448343c401b031417b972b0647df6d6717fca53b1ea458fe41eb4191.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c9123fd8186ff1f81f8631ebbf7e1d58&sv=3) **\[Attachment\]**.
    
    *   An attached file must always be accompanied by a comment (e.g., "View the attached").
        
    
3.  To reassign a task to a different user, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-a600f51d86cbf3c02c36ea5cf311867b2bb4cf89%252F00a06da7505c774dc36762a66bcd169b1d62cb35af729e3383729755194758fb.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b47095dd6af79cb390e303defaefd455&sv=3) **\[Edit\]** next to _Assignee_.
    
    *   Choose the account from which the new assignee will be selected, then select the new assignee.
        
    *   Select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-4e2eb56f7a0317e4afab899bcc5728b41c936979%252F156d94b4eae47d787101ad70424e4db735832f2f96a09cbe3ccfa3e08e408b05.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=2d3c3e6c4620be83d2c05ba05fb015a3&sv=3) **\[Check\]** to finish reassigning the task.
        
    

**Note:** Anyone can add comments to a task, however, only users with the the _Manage Tasks_ [account permission](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/invite-and-manage-users/understanding-user-management-as-a-brand)
 can reassign tasks.

#### Set task notifications[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/manage-tasks#set-task-notifications)

You can configure your impact.com notification settings so that you receive an alert whenever you're assigned a task or whenever an update occurs on a task you've created or been assigned to. Learn how to manage your account [notification settings](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/account-administration/account-settings/notifications/manage-your-brand-notification-settings)
.

# Modify Which Partners are in a Group | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/modify-which-partners-are-in-a-group.md)
.

Once you've created partner groups, you can add or remove partners as needed. To bulk edit your partners, see [Bulk Edit Partners in a Group](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/bulk-edit-partners-in-a-group)
 for further instructions.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Partners →** [**Partners**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/engage/mediapartners/view-mediapartners-flow.ihtml)
    .
    
    *   Alternatively, if you have a creator program, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-230534471fef5f40808e921e41ee44e4a06ded03%252Fe6cb9548999afdc1ed3ce4942e4cb5b45b5cecbd323267aac2a7cd1915fccc09.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a008b31e7dcd0623453accf7768aa9c3&sv=3) **\[Engage\]** → **Partners →** [**Creators**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/engage/discover/radius/fr/partner_ui.ihtml)
        **.**
        
    
2.  Hover over a partner’s name in the table and select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-9fd92d4378edf839f6a1f6fc0beaa1660b3892b5%252F69842c54f03332c1e2eed608d72b25ad10248384a914164a7255a1e94dc76ee2.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=451b9a3b07abfab620cb23b45c6ff74e&sv=3) **\[Unchecked boxes\]** next to the partners you want to add to your group.
    
    *   If you have a creator program, you need to first revert to the old UI. Select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FPORUgP0qyEEctyFkbw08%252Factions.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2d406300-0455-4291-8028-aed6300b6e3c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0154d8e72cfe696c92f3a0f6c925d560&sv=3) **\[Menu\]** in the upper-right of the screen, then select **Revert to old UI**.
        
    
3.  To modify which partners are in a group, follow the instructions in the tabs below, then select **Apply**.
    

Add partners

Remove partners

1.  Select the **Group names** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** above the table, then select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-9fd92d4378edf839f6a1f6fc0beaa1660b3892b5%252F69842c54f03332c1e2eed608d72b25ad10248384a914164a7255a1e94dc76ee2.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=451b9a3b07abfab620cb23b45c6ff74e&sv=3) **\[Unchecked box\]** next to the groups you want the selected partners to be in.
    
    *   If you see a ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b79ded6a1ff11f544ab54c877665a0bdbe8fd2ac%252F9deedb97f6bbb18043e811f271fd1a25997c5bb01f85484476d0624be20870c4.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8b39c3b77aec2488bc78e2ee2efcd30b&sv=3) in the _Groups_ ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-df93ac0f80fc5cece7983980a5ce3fbcf19aabce%252F3ef9d737089bc07dbb7e4fd41a97edfbaec261e4f55ab3a1666f3daf957f69c3.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7156f55b40b1371c0823c4c12cd99f7a&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**, then at least one of the partners you selected is not in the same group as the others.
        
    

1.  Select the **Group names** ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** above the table.
    
2.  To remove partners from a group, de-select the ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FwXho9QKP3e4iU4ZKUSJ5%252F167ebb8cc30303.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Da046e9f0-63aa-46f6-8a35-f73973fd326c&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=da58aac3e10a80e67d81ac893e14f6f6&sv=3) **\[Checked box\]** next to the group from which the selected partners should be removed.
    
    *   If you see a ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b79ded6a1ff11f544ab54c877665a0bdbe8fd2ac%252F9deedb97f6bbb18043e811f271fd1a25997c5bb01f85484476d0624be20870c4.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8b39c3b77aec2488bc78e2ee2efcd30b&sv=3) in the _Groups_ ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-df93ac0f80fc5cece7983980a5ce3fbcf19aabce%252F3ef9d737089bc07dbb7e4fd41a97edfbaec261e4f55ab3a1666f3daf957f69c3.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7156f55b40b1371c0823c4c12cd99f7a&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]**, then at least one of the partners you selected is not in the same group as the others.
        
    

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fm8l3RTDPXFfHWJkL9q5c%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-21%2520at%252017.54.02.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df85a2f6a-94b6-424c-a417-a42b11da0d37&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1235705bb9b9d45dfed2e165f050479d&sv=3)

#### Add prospective partners to groups[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/modify-which-partners-are-in-a-group#add-prospective-partners-to-groups)

You can add partners you've [marked as prospects](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/performance-program/recruiting-partners/prospective-partners/mark-potential-partners-as-prospects)
 to groups as well.

1.  From the left navigation menu, select ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FLGBlwxuVWsrxNsjYUMjf%252Fdiscover-v2%2520%281%29.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8a0e56eb-ff89-4046-8e72-ad2b503f8bf9&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=bdd59e861496a228e266235710ed1272&sv=3) **Discover** → [**Prospects**](https://app.impact.com/secure/advertiser/discover/radius/fr/partner_prospecting.ihtml)
    .
    
2.  Select a prospect, and from the slide-out, navigate to _Groups._
    
3.  Type the group's name and select **Save**.
    

**Important**: Ensure you type the correct existing group name when adding prospects to groups as inserting a new group name will result in a new group being automatically created.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fq54RtNU0OwBo2XXzeUOb%252FScreenshot%25202026-04-23%2520at%252013.06.55.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D3da5248b-3bbf-45a3-b4df-c1a667ab0e33&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=08e18b295e42782f3be5b7f940e56871&sv=3)

[PreviousBulk Edit Partners in a Group](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-groups/bulk-edit-partners-in-a-group)

# Overview of the Partners UI for Brands | I'm a Brand | impact.com Help Center

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://help.impact.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-management/overview-of-the-partners-ui-for-brands.md)
.

[Take the PXA course](https://pxa.impact.com/student/activity/1099688?sid=0c0e3e5c-54c9-4435-9bee-ebcdccb7f292&sid_i=0?utm_source=app.impact.com&utm_medium=owned-platform&utm_content=part-lp-351&utm_campaign=help-center)

Partners and brands have different user interfaces when they use impact.com. In this article, we will look at the partner user interface and get a brief overview of what they see when they work on impact.com.

![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252FB8EUwxfPk1YyWbv03fCf%252FScreenshot%25202026-05-25%2520at%252001.05.25.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D49ee23ef-be07-46b4-abe9-acd420e9cecb&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7bb66770485b553a7ed36bfc219b578d&sv=3)

#### Top navigation bar[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-management/overview-of-the-partners-ui-for-brands#top-navigation-bar)

The top navigation bar is where partners manage brands, run reports, find ads and tracking links supplied by the brands they’ve partnered with, see their account balance, and more. It also includes options related to a partner’s account, namely notifications, messaging inbox, global search, API & data feeds, settings, and more.

**Home**

The impact.com partner account home page is the dashboard, which provides an at-a-glance view of the happenings in a partner’s account.

**Discover**

Partners can manage their relationships with brands, view their program applications, as well as browse the Brands and Products Marketplace.

**Reports**

The _Reports_ screen holds all the reporting features for partner accounts; partners can see how their ads perform, how many actions their tracking links generate, and more.

**Content**

Here partners will find ads and tracking links for all of the brands with which they’ve partnered. They can also submit ad requests to brands here.

**Search**

Opens the global search-partners can input keywords or phrases to search all of Impact in seconds.

**Messaging Inbox**

This button opens the partner messages inbox. They can use this menu to communicate with you or other brands with which they are partnered.

**Create a Link**

Provides partners with quick access to the **Create A Link** widget for creating new [vanity links](https://help.impact.com/partner/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/tracking/tracking-links/create-and-manage-links/create-a-vanity-link)
. The create a link widget is a quick way for partners to create new short links and vanity links to promote the brands they work with. These links have tracking capabilities built right in.

**Notifications**

Partners can view notifications about their accounts, updates to their contracts, and configure their notification frequency settings.

**Balance**

This menu shows the current balance in a partner’s account, as well as a ![](https://help.impact.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F4048883401-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FwMLlMoFBtKJa8ptd3zaw%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-c2cb550b55c51ea1eeff0acbd6f437a03b621a2c%252F60d033ae8a04c14498d5229c3c0160ac05feea7f605dbd770741793c900469f0.svg%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3fed811e830570bef26b07d90bf98da0&sv=3) **\[Drop-down menu\]** with finance-related options, like an earnings report, withdrawals, funds transfer, and more.

**Note:** The [Finance Widget](https://help.impact.com/partner/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/dashboard/partner-finance-widget-explained)
 offers a detailed view of partners' payouts, including their daily earnings, pending payouts owed to them, and their current balance.

**Profile**

Partners can access their settings menu, view their impact.com profile details, or to sign out of impact.com from here.

#### Dashboard widgets[](https://help.impact.com/brand/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/reach-out-to-partners/partner-management/overview-of-the-partners-ui-for-brands#dashboard-widgets)

To learn about the various widgets on the partner dashboard, see [Partner Dashboard & Widgets Explained](https://help.impact.com/partner/what-would-you-like-to-learn-about/platform-features/dashboard/partner-dashboard-and-widgets-explained)
.
